# pcrec Project Plan

Working plan derived from ../../APPROACH.md. Milestones M0–M7 mirror APPROACH §9. Rewritten 2026-10-12 ([PLAN-LINT] step 2, docs/dev/lanes/plclean_report.md) and normalised to the one row format the Policy section defines ([PLAN-LINT] step 3, docs/dev/lanes/planlint_report.md); the triage that drove it is docs/dev/reviews/plan_triage_2026-10-12.md.

## Policy

**Row format** (checked by `scripts/plan_lint.sh`; it is a section of `make test` as `test-plan-lint`, and an opt-in commit gate: `make hooks HOOKS=pre-commit`). One row per ID, a head line then attribute lines indented two spaces under it:

    - [ID] STATE:<not-started|started|blocked|deferred> — <title, at most 100 chars>
      filed: <ISO date> <who>
      status (<ISO date>): <ONE line, at most 300 chars, rewritten in place, never appended>
      next: <the next concrete step>     (or `trigger:` — the D77 measurement that would start it)
      refs: <design notes, reports, K and D numbers>     (optional)
      <charter: at most 6 lines of at most 1000 chars — the owner's words and the problem, no history>
      - [CHILD-ID] STATE:<state> — <title>     (a child is nested by two spaces)

`next:` or `trigger:` is required for `not-started`, `blocked` and `deferred`, optional for `started`. `status` is the only volatile field; its ISO date is how a stale `started` row is found (`scripts/plan_lint.sh --stale`, a report to run by hand at wake, never part of `make test`). History does not live in a row: it goes to `docs/dev/dev_journal.md`, a lane report, or `docs/dev/plan_completed.md` (a row's older status stack is moved there verbatim), and `refs:` points at it. A charter that outgrows 6 lines moves its long form to `docs/design/plan_row_bodies/<ID>.md`, which `refs:` names; every such pointer must resolve and every body file must be named by a row here or in plan_completed.md.

**A finished row LEAVES this file.** Move it verbatim to `docs/dev/plan_completed.md` in the same commit — there is no `STATE:completed` here and no stub or tombstone; `grep -n '\[ID\]' docs/dev/plan.md docs/dev/plan_completed.md` finds it. Cite a row by `[ID]`, never by line number.

**Sections.** Active (started and blocked rows; [OPTLOOP] and [MEMFN] carry their children), Candidates ([OPTLOOP]), Backlog, Boonies, Deferred. Candidates are the next cycle's measured candidates (D137, Frank 2026-09-30, lane parkmeas): every optimization row that sits outside the umbrella, each carrying its evidence so far and the measurement it needs; the cycle's selection is BY MEASUREMENT (D119) and the D125 hold on opening the next cycle is unchanged. Per-need briefs and bench I-notes: docs/dev/lanes/parkmeas_report.md. Live builds stay where they are and are NOT candidates: [OPT-LITSCAN], [UCP], and the D135 rung. Backlog rows are chartered or filed by Frank or the manager with no milestone and no trigger yet, and each starts on his word; Boonies are far-future concepts, unscheduled, started only on his word; Deferred rows were tabled by an explicit ruling and each names what re-opens it.

**Find work** (anchored; a bare `grep STATE:started` also hits prose; never hard-code a count here):

    grep -nE "^[[:space:]]*- \[[^]]*\] STATE:started" docs/dev/plan.md
    grep -cE "^[[:space:]]*- \[[^]]*\] STATE:not-started" docs/dev/plan.md
    grep -c "STATE:completed" docs/dev/plan_completed.md

**Queue discipline (Frank, 2026-08-12).** The BOONIES TIER sits well after the general work: boonies rows are PARKING SPOTS, not queue positions, recorded so nothing is re-derived and started only on Frank's word. A boonies row growing substeps while spine rows sit not-started is the smell this note exists to stop. Remaining spine: M3 streaming, then M7 hardening (M4, M5, M6 complete); optimization work runs as [OPTLOOP] (D119/D144), not as a spine milestone.

**Rules.** Update the STATE tag and the `status` line in place when a step changes state; expand a milestone into substeps only when work on it begins. Process policy (lanes, boxes, closing a session) lives in decisions.md (D86), the root CLAUDE.md and the pcrec-manager skill, not here.

  - [OPT-REVEND] STATE:started — REVERSE-FROM-END SEARCH
    filed: 2026-10-05 (first appearance in plan.md, git; author unrecorded)
    status (2026-10-26): L0 c85a1aac, L1+L2+stage 2 7388f1c0 (abi 73), B3 match-end mark 206cf0a0b (abi 78; forms one-seed/tagged/two-seed, endmark.md §R); B4 e842fe537 is [COMPOUND]'s driver. No bench cell in the population yet
    trigger: (D77, not met) a bench cell in this population — an `anc-tail` family (`\d+$`, `\s+$`, `\w+\z`, `[^/]+$`, `.*\.txt$`; matching and non-matching tails on a long subject) showing pcrec-vs-peer at rank — or a user/real-world pattern census naming the idiom (the bench's `wild-*` validators are all…
    refs: docs/dev/optloop/revend_census.md; tests/revend/stage2_captures.rxt; docs/design/locate_finish.md; docs/dev/lanes/rewtwin_report.md; docs/design/endmark.md; decisions D62 D77 D137 D156; known_issues K49 K50 K103
    for a pattern every match of which ends at `n` (`\z`) or at `n`/`n-1` (`$`, `\Z`), seed the existing reverse DFA at `n` (and at `n-1` when `s[n-1]=='\n'`), run it backward until it dies or reaches `search_from`, and take the smallest accepting position `s*` as the match START; then run the artifact's existing anchored entry once from `s*` for the end (leftmost-first can still choose `n` or `n-1`) and the captures (the VM over the found span). This REPLACES today's O(subject) forward pass (an end-pinned machine cannot stop before `n`; the skip loop helps only while parked in state 0) by O(match) work, on matching AND non-matching subjects.
    EXACTNESS: every match ends in {n, n-1}, so the starts of all matches are the starts of matches ending there and `s*` is the leftmost start (the same argument the shipped forward-then-reverse protocol uses at the forward end); the end is never read off the reverse walk. WHERE IT DOES NOT HELP: start-anchored `^...$` (class S: one attempt at 0, D77's `(?:P)\z` whole-subject idiom), bounded width on byte encodings ([OPT-ENDWIN] already clamps the start window, 24 ns on 1 MiB; the D77 "final-byte skip gap" is moot there), `(?m)$` (pins nothing to the end, D62 control 3), VM-only artifacts (no reverse machine), `\G`, a trailing lookaround, and a leading unbounded repeat whose run IS the subject (`.*x$` on one huge line: no worse, no better). Under utf8 [OPT-ENDWIN] declines (K49/K50); a reverse walk is the natural replacement and must discharge the same boundary obligation.
    D77 LINEAGE: D77 declined an end-anchored generation AXIS and named the gap "a FOLD ON THE IDIOM (the skip loop reasoning about `\z`)"; [OPT-ENDWIN] built that fold for the bounded half; this is the UNBOUNDED half, a different mechanism (a seeded walk, not a clamp), recorded under [OS-4]'s measure-the-split charter. CENSUS (4,857 patterns, 345 bench + 4,512 corpus blocks, instrument cross-checked 0/0 against the shipped end_window fact): 22 bench byte + 4 utf8 and 733 corpus rows are end-pinned; the bench's are 17 start-anchored + 5 bounded-width (all at the floor, 24-454 ns vs `jit` 51 us-1.2 ms) and ZERO start-unanchored unbounded; the corpus has 33 distinct (47+1 rows, 32 DFA-routable), all anchors/view correctness witnesses.
    Scratch-tier Linux: today's linear scan on the idiom costs `\d+$` 0.87, `\w+$` 3.15, `\s+$` 2.5-3.1, `[a-z]+\.txt$` 3.2 ns/B (0.9-3.4 ms per MiB) against 40-160 ns for the same shapes bounded and windowed. TRIGGER (D77, not met): a bench cell in this population — an `anc-tail` family (`\d+$`, `\s+$`, `\w+\z`, `[^/]+$`, `.*\.txt$`; matching and non-matching tails on a long subject) showing pcrec-vs-peer at rank — or a user/real-world pattern census naming the idiom (the bench's `wild-*` validators are all `^...$`). First owed step when it fires: the hand-twin (replace the emitted forward loop by the seeded reverse loop) with answer-identity over the `end_window.rxt` subjects.
    [REVEND-WALK-FORM] ANSWERED 2026-10-12 session 110 (lane b3rev, archived with this cleanup; docs/design/endmark.md section R + endmark/b3rev/timing/): form W — seed views resolved before the loop, first step peeled per seed; the per-iteration view probe is the whole gap (+0.93 ns/call), T/W 1.47-1.90x, 0 wrong over 90.96M cells; B3 adopts W for its walks.

    partition a pattern's arms by WHERE a match of that arm can lie — START-pinned (`^`/`\A`/`\G`, and `(?m)^` = line-start-pinned), END-pinned (`$`/`\z`/`\Z`, `(?m)$`), FREE, and arms whose cheapest LOCATOR differs (different necessary literal or start set per branch) — give each class its own cheap candidate-start locator (one attempt at 0; a reverse/end-anchored pass from n; a line-start scan; the free arms' unanchored DFA/prefilter), take the LEFTMOST candidate across classes, and FINISH with the full machine at that start so leftmost-first priority among arms is preserved. It is a LOCATOR that is a UNION over arm classes in D156's locate x finish frame, not a new engine.
    MEMBERS it subsumes: [OPT-ATTEMPT-SPLIT] (the start-pinned case; concat-sqli, ~25 ms derived, exact twin built, split object 0.92-1.21x, fixes.md cand 7); D151 addendum 6 / r1-rinner-g2 F2's deferred per-branch-landmark unlock; [OPT-REVEND]'s end-anchored pass as the END class's locator.   filed: 2026-10-04 Frank

  refs: memfn/docs/requests.md; memfn/docs/journal.md; docs/design/compare_stack.md; studies/simd1; docs/design/memfn/; docs/design/memfn/integration.md; decisions D82 D91 D146 D147; known_issues K82
  a general-purpose, independently valuable project of byte-search/compare kernels (memchr2/3, memrchr, byte-set membership, ranges, caseless run find, multi-literal, run verify — the menu is the requirements' to fix), x86-64 + aarch64/NEON, whose kernels pcrec can later EMIT into self-contained generated code. Named generally on purpose ("pcrec-simd is too specific"); working name `memory-functions`.
  Frank's sequencing: (R1) REQUIREMENTS FIRST — needs derived from pcrec's search-site inventory (docs/design/compare_stack.md §2), D91's two budgets, [OPT-SIMD]'s ISA/dispatch notes, studies/simd1, the bench's class-shape census, AND a binding-form criterion: when a kernel may be a FUNCTION CALL (libc-style, PLT/ifunc, ~3.5 ns/call measured by lane k82cost), an INLINE call (static inline / header-only), or INJECTED code (emitted text specialized per pattern) — the K82 short-call term (~10 ns pair-arm floor, two memchr calls) is the motivating case, "it shouldn't have this 10ns overhead"; (R2) SURVEY existing open-source implementations against R1 (adopt / borrow ideas / build) — "it doesn't make sense to blow a bunch of time reinventing the wheel; at a minimum get ideas"; (R3) findings to Frank, who decides the way forward (adopt, fork, or a NEW SEPARATE REPO — the scope mandate is extended only on that ruling).
  Deliverables live in docs/design/memfn/ until then. If built, get it underway early so [OPT-SIMD] is not waiting. Current contract: docs/design/memfn/integration.md (revision 4.9); other deliverables docs/design/memfn/. Cross-notes: [SIMD-META], [OPT-SIMD], D91, D82, D146, D147, K82 (Q7).
  - [MEMFN-ROWCON] STATE:started — every kit row checks its own contract
    filed: 2026-10-07 Frank
    status (2026-10-07): OPENED 2026-10-07 by Frank in the kit session, the general fix after K96; kit notice in memfn/docs/responses.md f3a0fbca on lane/memfn-g2x
    refs: memfn/docs/responses.md; decisions D6 D144 D146 D152; known_issues K96
    Each kit row declares an allowlist of the site fields it honours. One shared gate declines everything else, so a contract field added later is declined by default rather than silently served (K96's shape: R4c's ofsskip arm served sites with a floor or a non-n miss it could not honour). Decisions become visible: which row was chosen, and why the others declined. Each row has a reach floor. Flow: read-only audit (kit, sonnet, running 2026-10-07) → light design → D6 panel → build. ZERO-MOVER for pcrec. If the design needs a pcrec-side explain/trace hook, it arrives as a kit request. Sibling of a family: the kit's row selection is a first-match table (D152), and this adds the per-row admission column it lacked.
    Frank's constraint (2026-10-07, given in the kit session): decisions move into memfn in the future, so the explain/visibility record is KIT-OWNED (a per-site decision record behind a kit API). Its shape is chosen so that [START-TABLE] C1's PCREC_CAND_TRACE site-key records can MIGRATE into it when the planner moves into the kit. So [START-TABLE] C2-C7 should keep the trace records in the literal-site-key form, ready to migrate. No pcrec request (R-6) is expected now. Q-ROW-1 RULED by Frank (2026-10-07, in the kit session): (1) NO SILENT DEFAULTS. Every value a kit row reads is stated with a named token (e.g. MF_MISS_N), and an unstated value is a loud refusal. This brings a later ZERO-MOVER pcrec request (R-6): pcrec's hook builders state MF_MISS_N, "0", etc. explicitly. (2) SNAPSHOT.
    Each delegated site evaluates each caller hook ONCE into a reserved `mf_` local, parenthesized (closing the precedence, double-evaluation, macro, mid-site-mutation and capture hazards). This is a pcrec ABI EVENT at every delegated site (G1 at both layers; deny `--memfn=no-snapshot`). It sequences through the manager among the START-TABLE bumps, with a bench inbox notice at landing. ROWCON's rev 2 hosts C2's cand_rows[] on paper with no special cases; analyses[] and [POSS-CTX-TABLE] are declared not hosted (not first-match). Rest RULED by Frank (2026-10-07): Q-ROW-4 AGREE: D146's charter widens, the kit hosts the general first-match table engine as an MF_NS utility, and pcrec tables may adopt it per table as an offer after C7. Q-ROW-6 AGREE: the snapshot has NO public deny; G1 compares against the pre-snapshot commit (a D144 item-4 exception for correctness changes).
    This SUPERSEDES the `--memfn=no-snapshot` deny above. Q-ROW-1/5 NARROWED: "a row may USE only a value the caller STATED"; unused fields are wildcards. So R-6 shrinks to stating only what the chosen rows use (e.g. MF_MISS_N on valued handoffs); the kit sends the list after its rev 3. Rev 3's pcrec-facing fixes: routes==0 is a definition error (on main it means DFA-only), a typed test thunk keeps pcrec's bool predicates as-is, an on_none hook instead of abort-on-none, and no second route carrier. SCOPE NARROWED by Frank (2026-10-07): "I don't want this to turn into a solution without problem scenario." BUILD NOW only the K96 generalization: per-row allowlists of USED fields, one shared decline gate, visible decline reasons in the kit trace, per-row reach floors, and the stated-value rule (R-6 stays small).
    HELD: (a) the general table engine; Q-ROW-4 stays ruled, but it builds only when a real table wants to adopt it, and the cand_rows[] mapping stays as design evidence only. (b) SNAPSHOT (T3b); its trigger is a census of pcrec's actual hook expressions showing a non-primary/side-effecting/precedence-fragile hook, or a migration that introduces one, otherwise it stays filed with the census as its evidence. Next panel: a light re-check of the narrowed gate. HOOK CENSUS (kit, 2026-10-07): NO RISKY HOOK. Every value hook pcrec passes (s, n, lo, result, result_decl, indent) is a bare identifier, on_miss is always `return 0;`, and fn/table names are plain identifiers. SNAPSHOT stays FILED with memfn's probes/rowcon/hook_census.md as its evidence, and its trigger is re-checked at every migration step that adds or changes a hook. ROWCON brings NO abi event.
    Rev 4 (narrowed) is d0dd3f5e on lane/memfn-rowcon, with its light re-check running.
  - [MEMFN-ENTRYSINK] STATE:not-started — a third kit sink: a function-entry point for setup invariant to an outer loop
    filed: 2026-10-08 Frank
    status (2026-10-08): not started
    trigger: (D77) a SIMD form whose assembly shows per-call setup re-done inside an outer loop, plus a measured gain from a hand twin that hoists it, under D147 addendum 13's bar.
    refs: decisions D77 D147
    FILED, NOT SCHEDULED (Frank, 2026-10-08: "something we can look for at the point we think we might need it"). A third kit sink on `mf_use`, a FUNCTION-ENTRY point pcrec names at the top of the entry body, for setup that is invariant to a pcrec-owned OUTER loop but not a compile-time constant (e.g. a value from the subject length or end pointer, or an alignment prologue). Candidate sites: the pre-check in the attempt loop, find-all retry, the hybrid re-seed. Today the kit has only file scope (`mf_define`, `mf_flush_helpers`, `static const` tables) and the use point.
    Pattern-constant setup (`set1` of a literal, mask loads) was first assumed to need neither ("gcc hoists it or it is a constant"); CORRECTED 2026-10-08 (kit r9fu measurement, lane/memfn-r9 @ fbc4a410): gcc 15.2 rebuilds set1 broadcasts at every function entry, even from a `static const`, so entry setup is real per-call cost; the only candidate site today is the filed OFS run-pinned form. RULE (Frank): build SIMD forms WITHOUT it, then measure whether adding it moves the needle. TRIGGER (D77): a SIMD form whose assembly shows per-call setup re-done inside an outer loop, plus a measured gain from a hand twin that hoists it, under D147 addendum 13's bar. If built: a contract step (`MF_SITE_ABI` bump), with pcrec owning where the point is.

An ASCII/single-byte-fold FAST PATH inside the encoding residual `<prefix>_span_match_caseless` (caseless backreferences AND `${name}` variables under `-e utf8`): when both spans are ASCII, the compare is the kit's masked byte compare (a kit site, the byte-domain sub-loop of a character walk); otherwise the encoding's decode walk (N7U, retired from the manifest).   - [MEMFN-RTDISPATCH] STATE:not-started — runtime dispatch for SIMD forms
    filed: 2026-10-08 Frank
    status (2026-10-17): not started; SHAPE RULED (D155 add. 5): each ISA level a target-attributed part chosen by the compound controller; one-shot search checks per call, the iterator keeps the choice in its state; never an artifact static
    trigger: (D77) more than one architecture selected for runtime support AND a hot-loop SIMD form exists — possibly theoretical today.
    refs: memfn/docs/responses.md; decisions D77 D155
    FILED, NOT SCHEDULED (Frank, 2026-10-08, D155). RUNTIME DISPATCH for SIMD forms. Why: a 500 KB artifact must not need 4-5 whole copies, one per CPU. OUT OF SCOPE: per-arch separate artifacts (one lib.so per -march, selected at load) already work through static selection; pcrec needs nothing for them. SHAPE: per-site multiversioning — only the hot helpers are duplicated per level; the level set is named at compile time and need not cascade (e.g. "AVX-512 or scalar"); static and runtime selection share the same file-scope helpers (D155 Q-R9-6 amendment). DISPATCH BY FREQUENCY CLASS per site: INFREQUENT sites (pre-check, find-start; about once per search call) may choose at runtime; FREQUENT hot-loop sites never pay a hardware check — they take a static choice, the lowest common level of the selected set or a named most-common level. Each site states its class (e.g.
    PRE FUNC per call; the OFS re-seed sits inside the scan loop). TRIGGER (D77): more than one architecture selected for runtime support AND a hot-loop SIMD form exists — possibly theoretical today. If built, the class is a `freq` column in DELEG_SITES (D155 addendum 1, Q-R9-11). Text: memfn/docs/responses.md (lane/memfn-m7 tip).

- [MEASURE-SET] STATE:started — DECISION MEASUREMENTS AS VERSIONED SETS, loadable at compile time
  filed: 2026-10-13 Frank
  status (2026-10-23): M0 on main (measures row, measure_store/ zen4-grid1@1, --measures + config, stamp, --list-measurements); Frank ruled the stop-ranking study Q1 + Q3 (RULED lines below)
  next: the kit measures zen4 for Q1 (memchr, two-stream pair, Horspool-probe cards + verify) as zen4-grid1 rev 2, a night window gated on the step-0 census after its C1 build (slot via main); then M1 (test-measures recompute, answers/staleness views); the envelope default is M2
  refs: docs/design/regime_tables.md; decisions D149 D161 D162 D166; [LIST-TABLES]; [ART-MGR]
  A measurement set is the machine-side twin of the analysis object: one box/run/method version, values relative to the scalar walk's per-byte cost plus derived crossovers, provenance per row (method id+version, box, compiler, commit, tier scratch/official). Deliverables: the set format and committed store (gen-tables shape), `--measures=FILE|NAME` + config line, the per-artifact set stamp (abi event), `--list-measurements` (rows, provenance, reader rows), the derived generic envelope set, an install-time measurement tool (crossovers only, noise-gated). Open: the relative unit, analysis-format vs sibling, the tool's home. Answers never depend on the set.

  wrap the find-all advance (match_api.md §3.1 "Finding every match": `p = non-empty ? <prefix>_next_pos(s,n,end-1) : <prefix>_next_pos(s,n,start)`, the [K75]/D132 aligned rule) in an emitted macro or `static inline` per-artifact helper (e.g. `<prefix>_next_start(s, n, caps)`), so callers stop hand-copying it and the looping rule can change later without touching callers. TODAY: the spec carries the loop as prose a caller copies; three in-tree drivers copy it by hand (tests/assertions/gstart_findall.c:64, tests/encseam/findall_driver.c:45, tests/codegen/entry_shape_driver.c:75; plus docs/dev/lanes/f3search_driver.c).
  EVAL QUESTIONS: (1) macro vs `static inline` in the artifact's .h vs an exported function (header-inline keeps it free and type-checked; an exported symbol is an rx_info/[V-E] entry-pointer candidate); (2) helper for the ADVANCE only, or a whole step `<prefix>_search_next(s,n,&p,caps)` returning the next match — the latter is where future flexibility pays: replace_design.md §3.8 notes the §3.1 loop cannot express PCRE2's NOTEMPTY_ATSTART retry for empty-preferring patterns, and [OPT-HYB-RESEED-XCALL]/xcall.md §5 held a caller-owned cross-call state (`_search_ex` + `pcrec_search_state`) that would ride the same entry; (3) relation to [V-E]'s entry-pointer struct; (4) it is emitted scaffolding: an abi event + spec hunk (D76/D94/D80), and the drivers migrate to it in the same change. Cost S.
  LEADING SHAPE (Frank 2026-10-09: "could it return a boolean such that one could write while (wrapper(..)) ..."; manager): a whole-step boolean iterator — `rx_iter it = RX_ITER_INIT; while (<prefix>_next(&it, s, n, caps)) report(caps); if (it.rc < 0) /* gave up */` — `_next` = (p > n → 0) + `_search` + the [K75] aligned advance; the done-vs-gave-up distinction (§4's negative codes) rides in `it.rc`, never dropped; the iterator struct is where the NOTEMPTY_ATSTART retry bit and the xcall.md §5 cross-call hint would live later, with no caller change; `rx_iter` a fixed unprefixed ABI type (rx_ctx's family), `_next` a `static inline` in the artifact header.

- [VM-LEN-HOIST] STATE:not-started — VM: one length check per choice-free fixed-width stretch
  filed: 2026-10-12 Frank
  status (2026-10-12): not started; FILED 2026-10-12 by Frank, session 110: "it tests scan_position < subject_length then looks at a byte. then repeats that 5 times. could it not verify minimum (and in this case absolute) length at the start and skip the compares?"; Frank: "lets do it" — a MEASUREMENT first
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: decisions D142; known_issues K23
  in the VM, a choice-free fixed-width stretch of the program needs ONE length check at its entry (`subject_length - scan_position < W` -> fail) and its per-byte `scan_position < subject_length` tests drop; capture boundaries inside the stretch are offset writes, so its literal bytes can then compare as one run (today's literal-run super-instruction fires only on runs >= 3 and a capture splits `fr(a)nk` into 2+1+2). The minimum-remaining-length fact exists (K23's `pcrec_minw`) but prunes only before choice points (`RX_VM_PRUNES 0x0` on `fr(a)nk`). gcc does not merge the checks (each guards its own fail edge). FIRST STEP: a hand-twin timing on a few VM shapes (today vs hoisted check vs hoisted + merged compare), answer-checked, on a quiet box. Sibling: [PF-KNOW]'s whole-program skip (D142; Frank wants to revisit it "when things are calmer") and [MATCH-KNOWN].
- [MATCH-KNOWN] STATE:not-started — one general record of what is already known about the subject at each emitted point
  filed: 2026-10-12 Frank
  status (2026-10-18): not started; TRIGGER REACHED in design: [KIT-VERIFY]'s proof (D161 add. 16) is its first member (review r2 kvp-unlock B1, 12-member family listed there); design note after the kvcensus census; general form = partial evaluation over known facts (see [CAPS-KNOWN])
  trigger: the third consumer that would otherwise take a private path (the forest-for-trees threshold) — [VM-LEN-HOIST]'s build or [KIT-VERIFY]'s, whichever first; a design note then, after discussion.
  refs: decisions D120 D142 D161 D164; known_issues K23 K82
  ONE general record of what is ALREADY KNOWN about the subject at each point of the emitted matcher, consumed by every emitter, instead of each producer handing its proof to one consumer by a private path. Members today (the family, dispersed): the hybrid prefilter's proof of the window and bytes ([PF-KNOW], D142), the kit's proven facts (D161 add. 8/10), the minimum remaining length (K23 MRL), the known start ([START-LANDING]'s landing / end-minus-width), the match-end mark's distance set (D164), the handoff K landmark (K82/L1), the compound's floor attempt (F proved at the floor), [VM-LEN-HOIST]'s length fact. Distinct from [PATFACTS] (D120: facts about the PATTERN, memoized per compile): this is facts about the SUBJECT at a program point, a dataflow lattice over the program (bytes proven at offsets, length bounds, position pins, context known).
  TRIGGER: the third consumer that would otherwise take a private path (the forest-for-trees threshold) — [VM-LEN-HOIST]'s build or [KIT-VERIFY]'s, whichever first; a design note then, after discussion.
  ARCHITECTURE (Frank, 2026-10-18, r3 Q2 ruled): every mechanism is a FACT PRODUCER stating what it adds at a point (kit proof, exact prefilter, rev-end's pinned end, landing, MRL minw, D164 end set, anchors) into ONE keyed record; ONE static evaluator walks the engine program over the record (partial evaluation, see [CAPS-KNOWN]) and yields report / enter-past / resume / nothing. ORDER MATTERS (Frank): for `\d{10}$` the forward producer knows the digits and rev-end knows the end; if the digit scan is applied first the end fact is lost, so the evaluator sees ALL producers' facts before the runtime order is chosen (position-pinning facts ahead of scanning ones).

- [KIT-VERIFY] STATE:not-started — two-way exact facts between a search site and its consumer (verify request, proven facts)
  filed: 2026-10-12 Frank
  status (2026-10-21): U1 builds under [DD-11.5] (go 2026-10-21): Z2 BUILT on lane/z2build (cover row via the harness lever, cover_conj[] C1-C14, span_facts slice, tests/kitproof, S1130-S1149), heavy checks queued; B1 after kit B4; hold U2
  trigger: chunk 2's contract note must show where both directions fit (the flexibility criterion's test case); a build waits for a measured consumer re-read cost on real cells.
  refs: decisions D77 D157 D161; known_issues K82
  TWO-WAY EXACT FACTS BETWEEN A SEARCH SITE AND ITS CONSUMER, instead of estimating the consumer's cost. (1) pcrec -> kit, an extra REQUEST on a site: "verify span k". For `foobar` the kit may search for one or two bytes (`b`, `r`); with verify it first finds, then compares the whole span (memcmp or a class compare per position) and returns only candidates where the span holds. (2) kit -> pcrec, PROVEN FACTS returned with the kit's text: "these positions of span k are already matched at every candidate this site returns", so pcrec's consumer skips re-testing them (its entry state or VM pc advances past them). Today pcrec re-reads bytes the search already proved ([OPT-A]'s memchr-at-k + verify, then the engine).
  FORCES: (2) is a NEW kit -> pcrec channel (today the kit returns C text only); it carries facts, not methods (D157), and must be exact; a site whose consumer cannot use a proof still gets correct code. Consumer price was DROPPED in its favour (D161 add. 6): verification at the chain's last link replaces pricing false candidates. Neighbours: D161 add. 3 (the window search: verify is a span option, not a new interface), [OPT-LITSCAN] S4 / K82 handoff (a proven landmark already moves the resume point: the precedent for (2)), [SPAN-ISEL] / [POS-PARALLEL] (class-span verify forms). TRIGGER: chunk 2's contract note must show where both directions fit (the flexibility criterion's test case); a build waits for a measured consumer re-read cost on real cells.
- [CAPS-KNOWN] STATE:not-started — captures known from the pattern: tag-deterministic groups, partial plus VM resume (U1b)
  filed: 2026-10-18 Frank
  status (2026-10-22): census SCHEDULED by Frank ("not a priority"), after the kit's S3 report numbers; scope: rwquest + bench (not only the short-literal corpus), plus leading proven-span length per pattern (real patterns use spans like `http://`)

  ONE FINISH `report` row on the VM/hybrid routes with a group map, folding [PF-KNOW] det_all, start_landing U1 (fixed-margin captures) and kit_proof U1b. The fact is TAG-DETERMINISM per accepting state of the anchored machine (all paths agree on each group's offsets, or UNSET; `(a)|b` decided by the state reached). Frank: it can work PARTIALLY: for `(abc)(\w+)` group 1 is determined and group 2 needs "the rest of the VM", so the finisher writes the determined groups and enters the VM after the determined part (a compile-time VM state: no open choice, no skipped assertion, no meter change). `caps == NULL` callers are compound's CAPTURES-ON-DEMAND client.
  GENERAL FORM (Frank): PARTIAL EVALUATION, "running the vm virtually on static data": execute the VM program at compile time over the proven bytes, stopping at the first instruction needing unknown data (an unproven byte, an undecidable choice, a meter difference); the residual state (pc, offset, written groups, static frames) is the VM's entry. Whole-cover, DFA entry past a proof and tag-determinism are instances. Open: static frames for pushed choices; meters (D158/GIVEUP1); libpcre2 agreement on groups. First step: a compile-only census of how far the static VM runs, per pattern and per GROUP, over the hybrid/VM population (85 corpus + 3 bench fully fixed-offset already counted).

- [ENG-THIN] STATE:not-started — MRL clamp gating/thinning: decide per pattern or site whether the clamp is emitted
  filed: 2026-08-17 Frank
  status (2026-08-17): not started
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: tests/base/d27_k23_ambiguous_decomposition.rxt; decisions D18 D51 D137; known_issues K23
  MRL CLAMP GATING/THINNING (Frank, 2026-08-17, twenty-ninth session, ruled at the [M4.6d] accept-as-is decision — D51 addendum has the accepted +8% residual this row exists to shrink): decide per PATTERN — or per SITE — whether the MRL clamp is emitted at all. SOUNDNESS-FREE BY CONSTRUCTION: with or without the clamp the VM returns identical answers (the 202,458-cell [M4.6d] differential is the demonstration); gating errors cost budget exposure or overhead, never wrong answers, so this is pure measure-then-implement optimization. TWO HALVES, Frank's two questions verbatim: (1) ANALYTIC, compile-time — the k23_design.md closed-form step law (validated exact out of sample) gives a worst-case unclamped step bound per pattern; clamp only where that bound exceeds a threshold against the step budget.
  Boolean is-it-ambiguous gating is NOT sufficient — the +8% shape ([a-z]{2,4}){2,8}b IS ambiguous-class; the gate must be quantitative. Conservative generalization of the law is this row's engineering; when uncertain, KEEP the clamp (wrong-keep costs <=8% on a bad shape, wrong-skip costs an honest refusal, neither is unsound). Per-site refinement: clamp only quantifiers participating in nests whose bound exceeds threshold. (2) EXEMPLAR-STATISTICAL — owned by [ENG-PGO], linked: given representative subjects ([DD-13]'s exemplar file references are the designed carrier), tune the COST side where (1) says the clamp is not strictly needed; exemplar stats must never override the safety side (tail inputs are exactly what exemplars do not show; DD-2's budget refusal stays the backstop). If a caller wants both variants, OS-0's named entry points are the D18-compliant shape — no hot-loop dispatch.
  REGRESSION TRIPWIRES already in place: run_mrl_tests.sh §1b (the 8-step counter-rung acceptance cell) and tests/base/d27_k23_ambiguous_decomposition.rxt (89 blinded cells) — over-thinning that reintroduces a K23-class blowup fails both, loudly. Schedule: boonies-queued with a natural measurement window alongside [M4.6e]'s VM perf work.

- [ENG-DIRECT] STATE:not-started — direct-coded automaton for frames-1 VM artifacts (forced-VM x9 faster on a failing scan)
  filed: 2026-08-18 (first appearance in plan.md, git; author unrecorded)
  status (2026-08-18): not started
  trigger: MEASURED 2026-09-02 (bench O-14 section 2 / [OPT-VMFL]): the forced-VM route on frames-1 artifacts runs x9.0 faster on a failing literal scan; the selection question is [OPT-VMFL]'s.
  refs: decisions D12 D18 D45 D46 D56 D137; known_issues K7 K24
  MEASURED TRIGGER 2026-09-02 (bench O-14 §2 / [OPT-VMFL]): the forced-VM route on frames-1 artifacts — a direct-coded automaton by construction — runs ×9.0 faster on a failing literal scan (0.296 vs 2.661 ns/B) once the computed goto is gone; Frank: this IS the direct-DFA idea; [OPT-VMFL] carries its STEP 0 and its selection question; STEP 0 MEASURED 2026-09-02 (optvmfl_step0.md): the direct-branch-dispatcher twin on frames≥2 bodies is NOT a general win (3-4% on a scan-loop-dominated body, flat-to-slower elsewhere) — the ×9 belongs to the SCAN-LOOP SHAPE, which is this row's own claim, not to the dispatch — DIRECT-CODED DFA EMISSION (Frank, CROSS-NOTE 2026-08-30 (Frank, with [OPT-VMLIT]): direct-coded emission is where the DFA gets STRING EDGES — a linear chain of states whose transitions are singleton bytes emits as one memcmp instead of per-byte table steps; the M2.8 trie's non-branching chains (nfa.c:192) are the finder, [OPT-VMLIT] is the VM twin.
  2026-08-18, thirtieth session: "could we implement without state transition tables? ... aabbc sets up the table mechanism and the loop but really its just a memcmp ... (?:\d{2,3}\.){3}\d{2,3} could be a fixed loop of checking digits followed by period ... this is what i think of for dfa and for islands of dfa — no overhead"). Emit a DFA-tier pattern's verify automaton as DIRECT CODE — the program counter IS the state (re2c's model; lex -f, Ragel's goto mode) — instead of the rx_fcls/rx_ftr table loop: reducible automaton structure becomes structured code (sequences, branches, counted loops), byte tests become gcc-optimizable range checks/compares (the OPT-A spelling menu falls out of gcc switch lowering for FREE in this model, and the address-taken-label pinning largely disappears — plain structured flow), literal chains become the OPT-A memcmp lead as a special case.
  THE DEEP PAYOFF beyond constant factors: a bounded repeat in direct code is a COUNTED LOOP (register + constant code) where the table DFA pays STATES linear in n — the exact K7/a{n} family D56 just bounded; direct-coded counting re-widens the a{9795} narrowing by making the state-set population it guards simply not exist for these shapes (the VM's counter/cursor rungs already prove the loop form on the capture side). THE HONEST TRADE, which is why tables stay the general engine: dense/irregular automata mispredict as branchy code where a table lookup is branchless; large/irreducible automata blow icache and D45 compile budgets as code (the cap moves from state count to code size); so this is a PER-PATTERN (or per-island) selection by measured shape — D46 stamp+force obligations apply as a new selection axis when built.
  EVIDENCE MACHINE: [BENCH-CEIL] is this row's instrument — the hand-written ceiling arm's code IS what direct emission should approach, and the per-case gap table is this row's worklist and its acceptance measure. CROSS-LINKS: [ENG-ISL]'s islands, once determinized, should be EMITTED via this row's mechanism (the "no overhead" half of the island idea — record there); DD-5's switch-based emitter is the PORTABILITY cousin but still state-variable-driven — true direct coding eliminates the state variable where control flow carries it; the K24 counterweight (computed-goto unoutlineability) applies to any emission-model change and travels with this row. Sequencing: with the OPT waves, after [BENCH-CEIL] produces the gap table; unmeasured engine work is not scheduled (D12/D18).
  RULED A THIRD ENGINE TYPE (Frank, 2026-08-18, same conversation): the roster becomes table-DFA (current) / DIRECT-DFA / VM — with the VM potentially carrying direct-DFA ISLANDS — so when built this joins the ENGINE SELECTION axis proper (select_engine picks per pattern; ENGM_* gains a member; RX_ENGINE stamps a third value; OS-0's named-entry-point discipline applies), not just the emitter. On re2c, read by Frank at filing: it VALIDATES THE MODEL and is deliberately not the tool — its regex language is far from PCRE2 (lexer dialect, longest-match semantics). pcrec's shape is what makes the model usable inside a PCRE2 compiler: full-dialect parse + per-pattern selection routes only the shapes that fit.
  SURVEY LEAD for the capture question: re2c's TDFA (tagged-DFA submatch extraction, Trofimovich) is the literature for captures in direct-coded DFAs — the road by which direct-DFA could someday serve capture-bearing patterns without the VM; survey by measurement per OPT-A's rule when this opens.
- [ENG-TDFA] STATE:not-started — tagged DFA: carry match-position tags in DFA states
  filed: 2026-10-09 Frank
  status (2026-10-09): not started; FILED 2026-10-09 by Frank, session 103; unscheduled, a BIG FISH
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  Two uses, designed together. (1) START tags recover a match's start inside the forward scan with no reverse pass. This is the general form of [START-LANDING]'s landing: the DFA DOES restart at each position, but a restart folded into a transition never passes back through idle, so the scan cannot see it. Tags make every restart visible. Tags also handle the case where one minimized state merges an old live attempt with a fresh restart (`xa|a`, start_landing.md §2.3: a state-keyed landing gives 55,152 wrong answers). It subsumes the filed [START-LANDING-EXC] ("die-together", ≤0.58 ms of bench weight, start_landing.md §2.8). (2) CAPTURE tags give submatch extraction in the DFA, so capture-bearing patterns need not route to the VM. Frank: "once we do, we might also look at 'tagging' states that clearly show a capture group and thats a bigger fish".
  Literature lead: re2c's TDFA (Trofimovich), already named on [ENG-DIRECT] as the road to captures in direct-coded DFAs. Opens only on Frank's word. First step when opened: a study (the survey by measurement, OPT-A's rule) of the populations the capture half would move off the VM, against PCRE2 leftmost-first priority semantics (TDFA literature is mostly POSIX/leftmost-longest).
- [ENG-COUNT] STATE:not-started — large bounded counts on the DFA side
  filed: 2026-08-29 Frank
  status (2026-08-29): not started
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: tests/resource; decisions D45 D77 D137; known_issues K7 K41
  (Frank, 2026-08-29 ~00:5x, forty-fourth session: "I'd like an unscheduled plan item to look at options for these forms but right now I'm not sure it's worth it as they seem pretty edge case and there is more profitable ways to spend time"). UNSCHEDULED, not on the loop's queue; opens only on a measured need. THE FORMS: a huge bounded repeat that the DFA engine can only express as states — `[a-z]{0,30000}` is 30,001 states by nature, `a{0,25000}`, `a{0,20000}`; today the tests/resource K7 pin guarantees they COMPILE within D45's budget (a robustness fixture, not an endorsement), and r41 S1 (2026-08-29) priced them for the first time against a mechanism — [ENG-ABS]'s optional anchored machine added +46 % compiler CPU on exactly these shapes (24.3 → 35.9 s on `[a-z]{0,30000}`) and its artifact 1.32 → 1.98 MB, answered by giving the optional machine its own state ceiling.
  THE VM SIDE ALREADY HAS THE LOOP FORM: the bounded-repeat COUNTER RUNG ([ENG-BREP]/counter-K — one body copy per K iterations + a trailed counter), with [ART-SIZE] STEP 2 choosing K by size and [ENG-CLAMP] the deferred per-quantifier downshift. OPTIONS TO LOOK AT when this opens (no design here): (a) a SELECTION rule on repeat magnitude — above a measured count, a pattern whose size IS a bounded repeat is routed to the VM counter rung rather than a giant table (a selection outcome, stamped, on the [SEL-1] shape); (b) a hybrid COUNTING form — a DFA for the body plus a counter (a counting automaton), the general form, sized against the same size term [ART-SIZE] built; (c) leave it: the shapes are edge cases, the caps and the K7 pin bound the cost.
  MEASURE FIRST (D77): how many real patterns (bench, corpus, the K41 fuzz population) have a DFA-side count above the candidate threshold, and what they cost in states, bytes, compile CPU and throughput on both engines today — the number that would trigger (a) or (b). Related: [ART-SIZE] (the size term and the caps), [ENG-BREP]/[ENG-CLAMP] (the VM counter), tests/resource (the K7 pin), r41 S1.

- [ENG-LOOK] STATE:not-started — lookaround by product construction in the DFA engine
  filed: 2026-09-29 Frank
  status (2026-09-29): STEP 0 DELIVERED 2026-09-29 (lane lacens2, docs/dev/eng_look_census.md): 98 fixed-width k=2-4 occurrences (72 from one boundary-matrix file). Lookbehind grows 2-3 → 3-10 states; state count is NOT the blocker.…
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: docs/dev/eng_look_census.md; decisions D6 D27 D137
  (chartered by Frank, 2026-08-23 13:5x, on the manager's analysis; Frank's framing: "my concern is unnecessary special handling code and duplicate code paths. Ideally we implement this in such a way that it produces the 'best' possible code while not actually knowing about the special assertions it's implementing … is the one character optimization the best form, or merely one that works for the immediate need?" — it is the minimal instance, not the form). THE FORM: a lookaround over a regular body is a constraint on the SUBJECT, not the match — `(?<=L)` at p ⟺ subject[..p] ∈ Σ*·L, a property of text the forward scan has already consumed; `(?=L)` at p ⟺ subject[p..] ∈ L·Σ*, the same property for the REVERSE scan.
  MECHANISM: the PRODUCT of the main automaton with each assertion body's recognizer (Σ*·L forward; reverse(L)·Σ* in the reverse machine), folded in during determinization so an assertion is a predicate on the product state; bounded lookahead in the forward pass as a k-byte delayed acceptance; unbounded lookahead in the forward pass DROPPED (superset — sound for end-finding) and resolved in the reverse pass or by the VM; the context seeded from the bytes before startpos by running the component over the prefix (mechanism 4 generalized).
  TODAY'S assertions machinery (assertions_ design.md waves B/C: the previous-byte context bit in the state identity, the class-indexed accept table, mechanism 4) IS this construction for one-class bodies — it becomes an INSTANCE, then gets DELETED once DD-11 rebinds \b/(?m)^/(?m)$/\Z to their lookaround definitions and the identity gate proves the product's artifact byte- identical (or measured-equivalent) to the hand-built one; where it is not, that is the optimizer's next target, never a reason to keep the special code. REACH: every bounded-length regular-body lookaround becomes DFA-eligible — `(?<=foo)bar`, `(?<!\d)\d{4}(?!\d)` — not the four assertion spellings only. MEASUREMENT FIRST: state growth — wave B measured the one-bit context's composed worst case at 38,009 states against the 32,000 cap; the product with larger components is not free.
  Size the component automata on (a) the expanded assertion corpus ([M6.6.1]'s substitution driver) and (b) the real lookaround population from the PCRE2 testdata and the D27 corpus; the construction ESTIMATES BEFORE COMMITTING and DECLINES to the VM past the caps ([ENG-CUT]'s shape). SEQUENCE: after [M6.6] ships the VM semantics (the VM stays as the exact verifier and the engine for captures/backrefs inside bodies); design gate with a D6 panel; the expanded-corpus three-way self-oracle is this row's acceptance test as well as M6.6's. The hybrid's prefilter soundness argument (dropping lookarounds = superset) is M6.6's to state and this row's to exploit. NO one-character fold ships anywhere — Frank's ruling: it is the duplicate path.
- [ENG-CUT] STATE:not-started — the full cut construction: replace A_ATOMIC with a cut-free sub-automaton
  filed: 2026-08-22 (first appearance in plan.md, git; author unrecorded)
  status (2026-08-22): not started
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: src/ir/dfa.c; docs/dev/optloop/cycle1_analysis.md; decisions D4 D50 D67 D77 D137
  THE FULL CUT CONSTRUCTION (chartered by the [M6.4.1] design, atomic_groups_design.md §5.5, 2026-08-22; plan row added at the design's merge per R31 D4): replace `A_ATOMIC(X)` with an equivalent cut-free sub-automaton — run X's own priority-first-accept determinisation to fix, per entry state, the single endpoint the cut commits to, and splice that deterministic prefix into the enclosing machine (the primitive is src/ir/dfa.c's priority prune). Plugs into engine_m4.md §5.2's `discharge` socket and therefore BUILDS that socket's plumbing FROM SCRATCH: [TOUR-5] (2026-09-20) DELETED the scaffolding under D77, because since [M4.6] shipped the socket (2026-08) nothing registered a hook and the rewrite arm could not have served one anyway — it set a local `rewrote` flag without ever publishing a new root, which is the live defect in unused code this row used to cite.
  So this row now owns the analysis fixpoint, its round bound and the root-publishing path as well as the hook body; engine_m4.md §5.2 keeps the design record. D67's contract still applies: output born ANY_ENGINE, copied nodes keep stamps. SIZE ESTIMATE (analytic, unmeasured on real patterns): worst-case exponential, `|D(X)| × |D(rest)|` states against PCREC_MAX_NFA_STATES and the 32,000/10,000 DFA caps — the rewrite must ESTIMATE BEFORE COMMITTING and DECLINE past the cap; a declining rewrite falls back to the VM, i.e. to module atomic-groups. What it buys: capture-free atomic patterns from VM to DFA — the population is the cut-changes-the-language class MINUS what the free discharge rescues, and it is UNMEASURED on real inputs.
  EVIDENCE GATE (D50's shape, confirmed by the manager 2026-08-22 under the autonomous-run grant): build when a [BENCH-1]/[ENG-PGO]-class customer exists; the measurement that would open it earlier is that population's size and throughput gap on a real corpus. (gate re-pointed 2026-09-22: [BENCH-1] folded into [OPTLOOP]; a qualifying customer is now evidenced by an [OPTLOOP] cycle's analysis, e.g. docs/dev/optloop/cycle1_analysis.md, rather than a standing BENCH-1 worklist) The day it lands, H4 (the match-here entries' rule) is REOPENED (design §4.4 H5), and the lowering must never ignore atomicity (registry.c:615-622's named trap; sabotage S91).
- [OPT-A] STATE:not-started — algorithmic search optimization (rare-byte pick, memchr2/3, multi-byte literal search)
  filed: 2026-08-13 (first appearance in plan.md, git; author unrecorded)
  status (2026-10-13): origsurvey: dfa-hat `\b(true|false|null)\b` = 38.6% of R17-b's ALPHA loss; {f,n,t} lands ~30x more than a prefix window. Frank: the origin may be a SUPERSET class window ([fnt][rau]...); no reverse walk needed
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: docs/design/plan_row_bodies/OPT-A.md; decisions D21 D86 D137
  ALGORITHMIC search optimization, research is part of the work (pcrec is open source and pulling from other open-source engines is the point: survey before hand-tuning): rare-byte prefilter selection (ripgrep/Hyperscan pick the RAREST byte by frequency; pcrec chooses memchr only at exactly one escape byte, else a bitmap), memchr2/memchr3, multi-byte literal search (Two-Way/Boyer-Moore/memmem) instead of scan-to-a-byte-then-step, a Teddy/SIMD multi-pattern prefilter, reverse-inner and suffix literal selection, shift-or/bitap, and more (the leads in D21; full list in the full text). CROSS-NOTE 2026-08-30 (with [OPT-VMLIT]'s probe): gcc never inlines memchr — every emitted memchr is a PLT+ifunc call — so at HIGH anchor density the per-candidate call overhead is a real term in that 0.38-0.56 ns/B figure; STEP 0 should separate call overhead from scan cost before any SIMD design.
  FULL TEXT (long-form charter and history, moved 2026-10-12): docs/design/plan_row_bodies/OPT-A.md.
- [OPT-NEG] STATE:not-started — negated-class scan bodies for axis I (`not-equal`, `not-in-small-set`)
  filed: 2026-09-02 Frank
  status (2026-09-02): not started
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: decisions D77 D82 D137
  (FILED 2026-09-02 by Frank, fiftieth session, "apropos of nothing": "we do scans for chars, and classes separately. But do we check if the class is a single reverse like \N which we could scan as 'does not equal'?" — ANSWER, read off emit_dfa.c:4424: NO. Axis I's scan bodies are exactly TWO — `range` (a contiguous byte range; a positive singleton is a width-1 range) and `bitmap` (a 256-entry table walk) — so every NEGATED class (`[^,]`, `[^"]`, `\N` = `[^\n]`, `[^"\\]`) scans through the bitmap today, one table load per byte.
  OPTIMIZATION column, an axis-I addition under D82 (a new live-candidate body object, its own stamp value, deny/force flags, answer-identical): (1) `not-equal` — the complement is ONE byte: the edge's loop test is `b != c`, and for the UNBOUNDED run (`[^x]*`) the whole scan IS `memchr(x)` (the prefilter's DFA_PF_MEMCHR shape, :2451, applied to the edge body — find the first excluded byte); (2) `not-in-small-set` — the complement is 2-4 bytes: `b != c1 && b != c2` (or a switch), no table. D77 MEASUREMENT FIRST: a census over the corpus + the bench's four sets of scan chains by COMPLEMENT SIZE of their class (csv5's `[^,]*`, kv-quoted's `[^"\\]`, log fields), and one hand-twin timing of `[^,]*` as memchr vs bitmap on a long line. Sits on axis I only — no change to the chain criterion or the edge emission; queued with [OPT-EDGE] (same file region) and shares its census.
  CROSS-NOTE 2026-09-02: a candidate ISLAND BODY under [ENG-ISL]'s VM-in-DFA framing once [OPT-EDGE] triggers it — build it as an island shape shared by both engines, not as a DFA-only edge kind.
- [OPT-ALTHASH] STATE:not-started — A k-BYTE BLOCK HASH FOR WIDE ALTERNATIONS
  filed: 2026-09-02 Frank
  status (2026-09-02): not started; FILED 2026-09-02 ~16:4x by Frank, fiftieth session — explicitly NOT folded into current work: "one strategy for large alternations might be a partial hash table. Grab say four chars and look up state for dfa or something for vm."
  trigger: (D77) altwide@0.2's measured curve for the DFA route against width (today FLAT to 256 — the determinized trie is already O(1) per byte; the block hash buys a constant factor in bytes-per-step…
  refs: decisions D77 D137
  instead of walking a trie one byte per step at the fan-out, hash the next k bytes (k≈4) and look up (DFA) the state reached after consuming the block, or (VM) the surviving branch — the Wu-Manber block-hash idea applied at the alternation's head, a k-stride transition where the automaton's fan-out is widest.
  Design notes recorded at filing, not answered: (a) soundness on the DFA — a k-block lookup is exact only when the k bytes are fully consumed from that state with no accept or view inside the block; states with an accept within k steps, and positions with fewer than k bytes left, take the per-byte path (the same head/fall-through splice the scan edge uses); (b) priority is the DFA's already (determinized from nfa.c:192's priority-preserving trie), so the DFA side inherits it; the VM side must map a block to the FIRST branch in alternation order among those whose prefix matches, which the trie's rule-1/rule-2 partitions give; (c) the table is a perfect hash over the trie's depth-k prefixes (size = distinct k-prefixes, not 256^k), a miss = dead (DFA) or no branch (VM); (d) relation to the island framing: this is a THIRD dispatch shape beside the first-byte switch on [ENG-ISL] — the switch wins at small fan-out, the block hash where the fan-out at depth 1 is small but the prefix set at
  depth k is large (dictionary-shaped alternations: srt/pfx3/w-N above a few hundred). TRIGGER (D77): altwide@0.2's measured curve for the DFA route against width (today FLAT to 256 — the determinized trie is already O(1) per byte; the block hash buys a constant factor in bytes-per-step, not a complexity class) and for the VM route under the bigcap axis where the trie dispatch would leave a fan-out too wide for a switch. No measurement owed until Frank opens it.

- [OPT-D] STATE:not-started — no-impact space savings in the emitted artifact (dedupe identical read-only data)
  filed: 2026-08-28 Frank
  status (2026-08-28): not started
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: docs/design/pf_know.md; decisions D76 D77 D82 D137 D142
  NO-IMPACT SPACE SAVINGS in the emitted artifact (Frank, 2026-08-28: "no impact space savings. Eg if the class bitmaps are repeated (ex forward and back dfa) then keep one copy"). Deduplicate emitted read-only data that is byte-identical across the artifact's machines — the byte-equivalence-class map / class bitmaps shared by a forward DFA and its backward (reverse-scan) twin, and any other table the emitter writes once per machine when the contents coincide — so one copy is emitted and every machine's accessor block points at it. SCOPE: artifact SIZE only; answers, stamps and the match API unchanged (answer-identity gate + `make test-axes` are the controls); runtime cost must be ZERO, proven the [ENG-FORM] way (objdump of the hot loops + timing within spread — D82), since a shared table is the same load either way.
  The [ENG-FORM] representation objects are the natural site: dedup is a SELECTION over the candidate lists (a table candidate that resolves to an already-emitted twin), not a special case in a machine's emit path (memory `pcrec-general-mechanisms-not-special-cases`). Measure first (D77): a census of emitted tables per artifact over the corpus — how many bytes are duplicates, on which patterns — names the win before the code; a change to the emitted scaffolding is an abi bump with its four sites (D76).
  Low priority; queued behind the bench-gated rows EVIDENCE (D142, [PF-KNOW] Q2, docs/design/pf_know.md §8.3): the forward and reverse `byte_class` maps coincide on 59 of 59 hybrids that carry both machines (the other 15 are start-pinned and have no reverse machine): 256 B each, 15.1 KB over the population — the real table prize; sharing the DFA prefilter's class partition with the VM's bitmaps (refines 114 of 118) is refuted on benefit (~3.4 KB corpus-wide at two dependent loads per test).
- [XART-TABLES] STATE:not-started — cross-artifact table sharing
  filed: 2026-09-28 Frank
  status (2026-09-28): not started; FILED by Frank 2026-09-28, eighty-fourth session, D125: filed, UNSCHEDULED; trigger: the first LARGE GENERAL table ships in emitted artifacts — e.g. a Unicode class via [CLS-TREE] or [UCP]
  trigger: the first LARGE GENERAL table ships in emitted artifacts — e.g. a Unicode class via [CLS-TREE] or [UCP]
  refs: decisions D125 D137
  when several pattern artifacts linked into ONE program each reference the same large general table (a UTF class like \p{Xwd}, a fold table), it should be LINKED ONCE, not once per artifact. [OPT-D] is the within-artifact sibling (forward/reverse DFA twins); this is across artifacts. Constraint that shapes it: every artifact stays SELF-CONTAINED (no runtime dependency on pcrec, APPROACH.md) — so the mechanism must dedup at LINK time rather than move the table into a support library.
  Candidate shapes to evaluate then (not ruled): content-hash-named symbols emitted as COMDAT/weak/`selectany`-style definitions the linker folds (gcc `__attribute__((weak))` or a `.section .rodata.<hash>,"aG",…,comdat` group; darwin's equivalent is `weak_definition` coalescing); an opt-in shared-tables TU (`pcrec --emit-tables`) for callers who prefer one; linker ICF as a non-answer (it folds code, not generally rodata). Questions for the design: symbol naming stability across pcrec versions/abi (a hash of CONTENT + table-format version, so two artifacts from different pcrec builds only fold when truly identical); static-library and shared-object behaviour on both platforms; how the size log / [ART-SIZE] should count a shared table (once per program, not per artifact).

- [POSS-CALL-COPY] STATE:not-started — PER-CALL-SITE POSSESSIFY VERDICTS
  filed: 2026-10-07 Frank
  status (2026-10-07): not started; FILED 2026-10-07 by Frank's ruling D154; UNSCHEDULED; D77: build when a measurement shows the cost
  trigger: a bench or corpus cell where a lost possessive mark in a spliced call target costs past the floor.
  refs: decisions D77 D137 D154; known_issues K93
  K93's fix (lane/k93fix, D154) gives each called group's body ONE verdict under its lexical context JOINED with every call site's, so a repeat is possessive only if safe after all of them. Frank's design recovers what that gives up: a non-recursive call is a call to the group's TEXT, each SPLICED copy gets its own verdict against its own follow (so `(a+)b(?1)a` keeps `a+` possessive at its lexical site while the `(?1)` copy stays greedy); bodies that stay SHARED (recursive, or linked by the splice budget / -fno-splice-calls) keep the joined verdict. Constraints (Frank): NO copies beyond callgraph.c's own splice decision; -fno-splice-calls keeps one body (the size lever). Shape: possessify runs per spliced copy, or splicing moves ahead of possessify (select_engine.c:1115 vs callgraph.c).
  TRIGGER: a bench or corpus cell where a lost possessive mark in a spliced call target costs past the floor. Population at filing: 0 bench movers (12 call patterns), 1 corpus mark lost (nocaptures.rxt:76, answer-identical). Sequencing: after K93 merges; [ART-POSS-ARMS] widens the same verdict, so whichever lands second re-derives.

  [FIND-TIE] (72e3ae41) moved the run reader's data tie to RIGHTMOST (D126 Q4 consistency). But `pf_run_applies_common` (emit_dfa.c:5357) requires that pick to EQUAL prefix_k's independent cost-greedy offset pick (`pcrec_prefix_ksets`), whose tie-break stayed LEFTMOST. On tied-rate run bytes the two disagree, and 48 of 165 artifacts left the run-pinned prefilter (165→117; e.g. `(a)(bb)(ccc)`, `SS`, `\(\)`). The answers are identical, but the prefilter FORM moved, which FIND-TIE's mover census never measured. Question: align prefix_k's tie with the run reader (one tie rule across both readers — the general-mechanism reading of D126 Q4), or let the identity clause tolerate equal-cost ties. STEP 0: the bench speed of the 48 movers (run-pinned vs the form they took). Floor re-derived to 100 with this mechanism named (docs/dev/lanes/rpfloor_report.md).
  2026-10-06 RE-SCOPE ADDENDUM (decsurvey §4.2 = start_table.md §6 Q10; manager recommends, Frank to confirm): from "align the tie rules" to ONE LANDMARK-CANDIDATE RANKING WITH ONE TIE RULE, read by the run reader, the pin and `prefix_k`. The identity clause also fails WITHOUT a tie (`\d\dxyz`: rarity picks z@4, the cost model x@2), so aligning ties alone leaves that case. Deletes run-pinned's identity clause (N1/N2). A form mover (answers identical), its own abi event after the start-table fold's C7, gated on D119's bench bar. STEP 0 unchanged (the bench speed of the movers) plus a mover census of the non-tie population (D-4's utf8 families and the byte-arm `\d\dxyz` shapes). RULED YES by Frank 2026-10-06 (D151 addendum 3, start_table.md §6 Q10).

STEP 0 READ (done): `prefix_k.c` reads no compile option, its inputs are the byte-rate and the `kset_walk` fact; if its cost model ever reads a compile option (the `--tune` dial) that effect enters via the fact's deny/input mechanism or stays outside facts as policy over the fact-provided ranking. - [CTX-PREFILTER] STATE:not-started — A LOOKAROUND LOWERS TO THE STRONGEST ONE-CHARACTER CONTEXT THE MACHINE CAN HOST
  filed: 2026-09-29 Frank
  status (2026-09-29): STEP 0 DELIVERED 2026-09-29 (lane lacens2, docs/dev/ctx_prefilter_census.md): 143 still-VM patterns carry a positive multi-char lookaround (only 6 bench-derived); 111/115 with a sound necessary byte are NARROW; the tightening is an INDEPENDENCE MODEL, not measured.…
  next: selected by measurement when an [OPTLOOP] cycle opens (D119/D137); no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: docs/dev/ctx_prefilter_census.md; docs/dev/ctx_prefilter_joint.md; studies/ctx_prefilter_joint/; src/ir/nfa.c; docs/dev/lookaround_census; decisions D77 D137 D153
  Today (src/ir/nfa.c:690) every A_LOOK becomes N_EPS in the NFA. A lookaround pattern is VM-forced, so the only DFA built from it is the hybrid's PREFILTER, which is the lookaround-free superset: sound rejection and a sound span start, but the lookaround contributes no rejection power (and Vm.mrl_win stays excluded). U2's A_CTX node carries a one-character context set. The general rule: EXACT when the body is one character (U2, the real DFA); for a longer POSITIVE body, a NECESSARY one-character condition, in the PREFILTER ONLY (`(?=abc)` → next ∈ {a}; `(?<=xy)` → prev ∈ {y}; `(?=\d+px)` → next ∈ \d), which is still a superset, only tighter. Negative multi-character bodies imply nothing and stay N_EPS. It does NOT lift the mrl_win exclusion (span end is still not an upper bound).
  As a T3 row pair (the prefilter-only approximate row after the exact row), it is a first-match table with no special case. STEP 0 (D77, Mac, cheap): a census over docs/dev/lookaround_census (493) + the bench's VM-only lookaround patterns of positive lookarounds whose first/last-character set is NARROW, plus how many false candidates their prefilters admit today. Build only if the census shows a population whose prefilter would materially tighten.

Shape: its own deny bit, placed before `pcrec_minimize_dfa` so minimization does the merge.

  filed: 2026-10-26 Frank + manager (from the `\s+$` discussion); a tracking row: related findings add a dated note here
  status (2026-10-26): filed to gather information; nothing designed or built. Today's rewrites are separate passes (altcls.c, atomic.c, possessify.c) with no shared mechanism.
  trigger: enough members/notes to judge a first-match rewrite table worth building (D-table style: run facts x follower facts x node options -> action, each fired row named in --explain, PCRE2 differential on/off)

  Members known (2026-10-26): (1) ABSORB-OPTIONAL: `X+Y?` / `X+Y*` with Y a subset of X -> `X+` (greedy/possessive only; no capture boundary between; e.g. `\s+\n?`). (2) ABSORB-EOL: `X+$` non-multiline -> `X+\z` when the configured newline is inside X (`\s+$`; lazy excluded: `\s+?$` on "a \n" differs; CRLF/ANY newline need the whole sequence covered). (3) Frank's earlier conditional anchor-rewrite table (option-dependent `$` etc.): mostly covered by A_CTX (D168), remainder belongs here. Possible future member: possessify (the disjoint-follower case; panel-repaired rule, leave in place until the table proves itself).
  Notes: (add dated notes as related issues surface)
  note (2026-10-26, lane rwsurvey, docs/dev/lanes/rwsurvey_report.md): no pass CREATES absorb-optional / `X+$` shapes on the 5,735 corpus + 351 bench dumps; constructed witnesses do (altcls: `\s+(?:\n|\r)?` -> `\s+[\n\r]?`, then A then B chain, so the table must iterate). Passes DO leave: altcls empty-branch alternations (`f(|rank)`, 107 corpus), possessify ATOMIC(REP possessive) (57). Only 29 outside-written patterns exist (6 carry a shape). More candidates: CTX-IMPLIED-AFTER-RUN (`\w+\b`), ALT-EMPTY normal form, MERGE-RUN (`[0-9][0-9]?`), REP-OF-REP, TRIVIAL-REP (`{1}`), DUP-ZERO-WIDTH/DEAD-PIN, REDUNDANT-CUT. - [ITER-BLOCK] STATE:not-started — FIND-ALL BY BLOCKS: SEARCH A BLOCK, RETURN ITS MATCHES, ELSE THE NEXT BLOCK
  filed: 2026-10-21 Frank (I2 discussion: "search a block for answers, if you find at least one, stop and return them, if you find none, go to the next block. that way the oversearch is bounded")
  status (2026-10-21): filed; nothing built. I2 ships with the batch doubling from 1 (bounds the early-break over-scan with no new mechanism); this row is the general form.
  trigger: [M3] streaming, or a caller asking for bounded latency per call, or I2's timing showing the 1-start costs a measured gain.
  refs: docs/design/findall_iter_i2.md (rev 2, sem-1); docs/dev/reviews/2026-10-21-r1-findall-i2.md; [FINDALL-NEXT]; [M3]
  Charter: `next_n` fills from matches whose START lies in the current block [p, p+B) (B a parameter, about 1-8 KiB per Frank; a setter on rx_iter like I1's; its default labelled UNMEASURED until timed; batch may start at 64); a block with no match moves on to the next, so the over-search past the caller's last wanted match is at most one block. Needs a caller-supplied start limit on every engine's search ("leftmost match starting before X", scanning past X only to finish a match that started before it): the start table's BOUND slot (start_max / attempt_max) is the existing mechanism to generalize, not a new scan loop. Semantics to state: a match straddling the block end belongs to the block it starts in; "returned < k" no longer means ended (state says).

- [MEMFN-POSCOST] STATE:not-started — PRICE A SET-FIND'S SCAN POSITION BY ITS SET'S FORM, PLUS SUPERSET COVERS
  filed: 2026-10-21 kit session (Frank to the kit: "file as items to follow up later; build as agreed now; put such refinements in if timing and survey suggest benefit"); filed in plan.md by the manager
  status (2026-10-21): filed; neither part built; B4 goes ahead as agreed. Counts (kit b4close2, manifest at abi 82): of 603 walk-routed set sites, 21 have a <= 2-term position inside their span (a floor), 408 have a <= 2-term superset hull with <= 8 extra members; timing still owed for the trigger.
  trigger: B4's regenerated mover manifest shows enough walk-routed set sites with a <= 2-term position inside their span (the B4-prep closing lane counts it), and grid/bench timing shows the gain.
  refs: memfn/docs/ideas.md 2026-10-21; docs/design/memfn/r17b_prime.md §1.3a R7/R7w; memfn/docs/responses.md (kit notice 2026-10-21)
  Charter: (1) a set FIND may filter at a cheaper position inside its span, then verify the costly head positions per candidate; the scan position is priced by rate AND by the form its set takes (V1 at <= 2 terms, `kit.terms.V1max`), not by rate alone. (2) a >= 3-term set may be searched through a <= 2-term SUPERSET filter, then verified exactly (e.g. `\w` through `[0-9]|[A-z]`, 5 extra members); a FIND filter only, never a proof for a REPORT site. (3) VERIFY order and form: a candidate's span verify tests its positions in an order priced by rejection rate per cost, not pattern order (today ofsskip's `verify_chain` tests ascending by offset; K82/R-1 showed lead order is part of a form's cost); each position takes its cheapest exact test (a word compare over literal runs, a set test, possibly a superset test first); proven offsets skipped as R-14's trim does. Part 3's trigger adds a verify-cost reading on dense-candidate cells.
- [MEMFN-UNBOUNDED-SPANS] STATE:not-started — STATE req_set / req_whole_run TO THE KIT AS UNBOUNDED-OFFSET SPANS
  filed: 2026-10-23 manager, from Frank's stop-ranking Q4 ruling (D176)
  status (2026-10-23): filed; nothing built. Kit side (one decider, the window family) is the kit's; open design question: does today's span carry a backtrack range or a fixed offset.

  Charter: pcrec states the required set and run it already knows (req_set, req_whole_run) as spans with an UNBOUNDED offset ("no offset"), e.g. `b` in `a.*b`, for presence only, and states the positions beyond a long literal's verify slice as cheap candidate spans, so the kit's priced stop choice can pick from the whole literal (RULED, Frank 2026-10-23, "Yes unless it has reason not to": default yes; any omission, e.g. a statement-size limit, is a stated visible reason, never a silent drop; same MF_SITE_ABI event). A bounded-offset span is a stop candidate; the use reads off the range, with no pattern-shape rules. Facts only (D157).

(B) green, re-pinned f6c230e5f

K128 would need a ninth with a slot rule (label renumbering plus a split guard/counter slot), and the 9 (?U)-possessive movers predate it. New datum: bref-rename bucket 328 on every axis (366 on 10-23, census 411, band +-30): 38 backref patterns left it; the re-base lane lists bucket membership first (KEEP=1 gate run).
- [SEQ-FOLD] STATE:not-started — FOLDED TEXT MATCHES: one fold table for full case, accent and look-alike folding
  filed: 2026-10-24 Frank ("The general mechanism sounds good"), from lane utfwant's survey
  status (2026-10-24): go (Frank); starts after every open item and its follow-ups complete; children are rows of ONE fold mechanism, full case folding first (demand), look-alike last (no outside demand found)
  next: a design note (coordinator design round): the 1:n fold table, then each child as its rows
  refs: docs/dev/utf_wants_2026-10-24.md (wants 4, 5; the NFKC finding); docs/dev/compat_fold_facts/; third_party/ucd-16.0.0 (CaseFolding.txt F entries); memory general-mechanisms; D26 D174
  General form (Frank 2026-10-24; widened the same day after Frank asked about n:1 and n:m): BOTH sides pass through the same per-code-point fold (code point -> sequence, generated from UCD) and the folded sequences are compared, so 1:n, n:1 and n:m (ﬃ vs ﬀi, ệ vs ẹ + ̂) are one case. The pattern side folds at compile time; the subject folds as it is read. Full case folding, canonical equivalence and compatibility folding are three data sets for it, not three features. Design questions: match ends inside one character's fold image (s against half of ß: refuse, as ICU does?), mapping offsets and captures back to the subject, combining-mark reordering for canonical equivalence (a bounded buffer; Unicode's stream-safe limit is 30 marks), classes and ranges, \b and lookbehind length, emitted size.

    filed: 2026-10-23 Frank
    status (2026-10-24): third child of [SEQ-FOLD] (Frank 2026-10-24); a pcrec novelty: no engine offers it and no request was found (utfwant)
    next: after [CANON-EQUIV]
    refs: docs/dev/compat_fold_facts/; docs/pcre2_compliance.md ((?r), (*CASELESS_RESTRICT), (*TURKISH_CASING) rows); [IFACE-PUSH]; SR-10
    A pattern literal or class member also matches every code point whose Unicode compatibility mapping (NFKC, generated from UCD like the case fold) is that character; with (?i) the case variants too. Not in PCRE2: a pcrec extension behind --features (SR-10). Syntax leaning (manager, to rule in the design): a whole-pattern option first (a behaviour-named option bit + CLI + .rxt attribute) and a start-of-pattern verb mirroring PCRE2's own fold verbs; a scoped inline letter deferred until a use case needs part-pattern folding. Design questions: 1:n mappings (ligature U+FB01 to "fi", like the case fold's 1:n arm), whether the equivalence is symmetric (pattern 'ｆ' matches 'f'), interaction with classes/ranges and \b, size cost of the widened classes. Test sibling: tests/codegen case-spelling identity (spellings equal under the fold compile to the same matcher).

- [UTF-PAT] STATE:not-started — UTF-8 AS THE PATTERN-TEXT ENCODING
  filed: 2026-09-05 Frank
  status (2026-10-12): M5.0 closed 2026-09-12 (all stages landed), so the "stage 2/3-4" wording below names M5.0's stages and is history; the row is unblocked and unscheduled. CHARTERED by Frank 2026-09-05, fifty-fifth session, UNSCHEDULED: "using utf as the Regex encoding.…
  next: starts on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: docs/spec/; tests/utf8/; decisions D27
  the contract, validated and tested. Frank's guess measured CORRECT at chartering: stage 2's pattern-char decode already handles raw UTF-8 pattern text under `-e utf8` — a raw `[α-ω]` (bytes 5B CE B1 2D CF 89 5D) emits the IDENTICAL machine to `[\x{3b1}-\x{3c9}]` (manager probe, artifact diff: only the provenance stamp/pattern-comment lines differ).
  What the row owes is everything AROUND that: (1) the CONTRACT stated in docs/spec/ (cli.md/registry.md): pattern text is interpreted in the PATTERN's encoding = the `-e` encoding (byte: pattern bytes are bytes — a raw `[α-ω]` under byte is legitimately the byte-class reading; utf8: pattern bytes decode to characters), including what an ILL-FORMED pattern byte sequence does (expect: clean compile error naming the offset, never a silent byte reading — MEASURE first, today's behavior unverified); (2) a VALIDATION SWEEP: raw-vs-\x{} artifact equivalence over a generated population (literals, classes, ranges, negation, case boundaries at 0x7F/0x80, astral, combining marks in literals), oracle-verified against 10.46 compiled WITH PCRE2_UTF on the pattern; (3) a D27-style corpus arm in tests/utf8/ (raw-spelling twins of existing blocks; the .rxt format's own file-encoding question — a `pattern` line carrying raw UTF-8 — is the manager's call per dd13b); (4) provenance surfaces: the
  pattern-comment/rx_info.pattern render for non-ASCII pattern text (today it renders \xNN-escaped — probably right, but say so in the spec); (5) registry/compliance rows if any construct's answer changes by pattern encoding. NOT this row: UCP semantics for \w/\d (stage 3-4 territory), utf8-by-default (a separate ruling if ever). Sonnet-sized measurement+corpus+spec; engine changes only if (1)'s measurement finds a silent-byte hole (then it is a miscompile-class fix, escalate).
- [UTF-RW] STATE:not-started — THE REAL-WORLD UTF REGEX HARVEST + THE CLASS-SHAPE CENSUS
  filed: 2026-09-05 Frank
  status (2026-10-12): its sequencing condition (M5.0 stages 3-5 landed) is MET — M5.0 closed 2026-09-12; the row is unblocked and unscheduled. CHARTERED by Frank 2026-09-05, fifty-fifth session, SEQUENCED after the utf build (M5.0 stages 3-5) lands: "go out and find some good real world utf regex for testing.…
  next: starts on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: tests/utf8/; decisions D27 D77
  Two deliverables from one collection: (1) THE HARVEST — sourced real-world UTF-8 patterns from NON-ENGLISH-LOCALE ecosystems, deliberately outside this project's own authoring bias: validation patterns from CJK/Arabic/Devanagari/Thai/Cyrillic/Greek/Hebrew codebases and frameworks (form validators, ID/postal formats, tokenizers, log/line parsers written BY speakers of those languages, not English-locale approximations), i18n test suites (W3C/CLDR-adjacent), combining-mark and RTL material — every pattern with PROVENANCE AND LICENSE recorded (REFERENCES.md-style citation discipline; no unlicensed corpus text lands in the tree), oracle-verified against 10.46 under PCRE2_UTF, landed as a tests/utf8/ corpus arm (not D27-blinded — a harvest is public material, the blinding lever does not apply; the D27 lever for this milestone already ran) AND proposed to the bench as a real-world utf SET (their syntax-census/[B36] machinery is the natural consumer — coordinate via the channel).
  (2) THE CLASS-SHAPE CENSUS over the harvest, which is the row's measurement payload (Frank, same day: real classes may hold MANY NON-CONTIGUOUS characters — "degenerate cases... which would need to be if-then'd. Perhaps a strategy is bitmaps over clusters. Needs real world examples"): per-pattern distributions of class interval counts, per-LEAD-BYTE cluster counts, lowered branch counts, VM chain lengths and DFA state counts, split by script family — the population that decides whether Frank's CLUSTER-BITMAP candidate (per-lead-byte continuation-set bitmaps — a 2-byte character's continuation space is 64 values = one 8-byte mask per cluster, replacing a chain of range tests in the VM lowering) is a real form-ladder rung or a shape real patterns never produce. D77 discipline: the candidate is NAMED here and BUILT only on the census's numbers.
  Standing evidence to weigh, not assume: the DFA side already cluster-shares aggressively (\p{L} = 648 intervals minimizes to 283 states — the byte-class equivalence map is doing the collapsing; the hazard is the VM route's linear branch chain, and any fix is a [FORM-CHAR]-family form question); form_char_step0's family-B result (byte-tier bitmap tables LOSE .text vs range compares on sparse ASCII classes) does NOT transfer unexamined — a continuation-cluster mask is a different size/shape trade at a different alphabet width. Sequencing note: the harvest wants stages 3-4 compiled (\p, Unicode fold — most real i18n patterns use them); a class/literal-only SUBSET is compilable at stage 2 and may be collected early if a lane is idle. Sonnet-sized collection + census; any resulting form rung is its own measured engine row.
