DEADLINE: this is a match-API design constraint and must be decided BEFORE M4's match-API freezes (same gate as PC-5). Semantics to document honestly: a callout-as- submatcher is OPAQUE to the automaton — atomic (no backtracking into it) and un-fusable, partitioning the pattern into [M4.0]'s DFA islands around call points.
  TENSION to resolve at design time: this row's pcre2_callout_block-exact mirror vs the aligned ABI — possibly reconciled by V-A's trampoline direction (the PCRE2-shaped block as a compat layer ON TOP of the aligned primitive)
- [FREESTANDING] STATE:not-started — the libc-free emission profile
  filed: 2026-08-28 Frank
  status (2026-08-28): not started; BOONIES TIER — Frank, 2026-08-28: "I think memchr requires glibc. If so, there should be a switch for a no glibc build which in our world is a simple scalar replacement on one of the forms for prefilter. File for boonies"
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: decisions D77 D82
  FACT CHECK first: `memchr` is ISO C (`<string.h>`), so every HOSTED libc has it (glibc, musl, newlib…); the target this row serves is a FREESTANDING build (`-ffreestanding`, no libc at all — kernels, bootloaders, bare-metal firmware, the "freestanding/embedded build profile" [M7.0] already names).
  SHAPE: one emitter switch (`--freestanding`, a generation axis with its stamp, [CHK-2]'s five things) under which the artifact calls NOTHING from libc: the candidate-scan forms that call `memchr` (the k=0 memchr form and [OPT-K]'s offset-set scan at k*) get a scalar byte loop in the same [ENG-FORM] slot — a SELECTION, not a special case — and the D82 bar applies (the scalar loop is a different representation object, measured, not a `#ifdef` inside the memchr one); every other libc reach in the emitted text is censused and replaced or refused: the emitter string literals today carry `memchr` (20 sites), `memset` (18), `memcpy` (16), `strlen` (18), `memcmp` (2), `malloc`/`free` (10/30 — the default frame/trail storage of the un-suffixed VM entries; under freestanding only the `_in` entries with a caller-provided buffer exist, and the un-suffixed ones are refused or emitted as thin `_in` wrappers over a static region, ruled at design time), and `--emit-main`'s driver (stdio) is simply
  unavailable. MEASURED on filing (the fuzz witness, a VM artifact with prefilter "none", split form): its .c + .h reach NO libc symbol at all and include only `<stddef.h>`/`<stdint.h>` — so a prefilter-less VM artifact is ALREADY freestanding-clean, and the switch's real scope is the DFA/hybrid artifacts' `memchr` scans plus whatever the un-suffixed entries' default storage and the driver pull in. The census of which sites reach a NON-main artifact (vs only the driver) is the row's first step; gcc's own `-ffreestanding` build of the corpus artifacts under the switch is the gate (a link with `-nostdlib` and a stub `_start`, or at least `-ffreestanding -fno-builtin` compile + `nm` showing no undefined libc symbols).
  Related: [DD-5] (`--std-c` portable emitter fallback — the same family of "emit for a smaller world" switches; the two should share their axis plumbing), [M7.0], [DD-14.FB] (the `_in` entries are the freestanding calling convention already). Not before the spine (queue discipline); no measured need yet (D77) — recorded so it is not re-derived. ABSORBED [DD-5] (was STATE:not-started; merged 2026-10-12, plclean [PLAN-LINT] step 2): — --std-c portable emitter fallback (switch-based) (R1 R-5)
- [BENCH-CEIL] STATE:not-started — the expert-C ceiling arm
  filed: 2026-08-18 Frank
  status (2026-08-18): not started
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: docs/dev/optloop/cycle1_analysis.md; decisions D52
  (Frank, 2026-08-18, thirtieth session: "for a set of (dfa?) patterns, i'd like to directly write performant C code to implement them and consider them one engine to compare against... ideally we should be able to match the performance but realistically i'm hoping we can get close or at least understand how far off we are"). For a chosen set of bench patterns, HAND-WRITTEN performant C matchers ride the bench as their own engine arm.
  WHY THIS ARM IS SPECIAL: unlike pcre2-interp/jit (different execution architectures), hand C shares pcrec's exact execution model — same compiler, same flags, same box — so the pcrec-vs-hand gap DECOMPOSES into attributable generator overheads (table dispatch vs specialized control flow, per-byte machinery vs wide compares/memcmp, prefilter choices), and the per-case gap table is the OPT waves' most direct worklist ("hand code does X, the emitter does Y" — the [OPT-A] literal-run and spelling-menu leads of 2026-08-18 are exactly the first two entries this arm would have generated).
  Rules recorded at filing: (1) v1 scope is DFA-TIER patterns (hand-writing a correct backtracking/ capture matcher is a different order of difficulty; VM-tier cases optional later, and a hand VM case would first need its semantics pinned against the spec); (2) hand code must be OBSERVABLY span-identical on the case's subjects — compare.sh's cross-engine agreement check already enforces this per case (INVALID, not timed, on mismatch), which is the correctness bar; (3) any technique a C programmer would write is allowed — that is the point of a ceiling — but each case documents its technique so gaps stay attributable; (4) same harness, same pinned-run discipline, floors optional (the arm is an INSTRUMENT like the prioritizer, not a gate — hand code regressing means nothing about pcrec).
  SCHEDULING (Frank's stated timing): after the main spine, with the benchmarking/perf-iteration phase — alongside [BENCH-1]'s build-out and feeding the OPT waves; a THIN early slice over today's a-m cases is cheap if wanted sooner. Authorship is expert-lane work (opus tier) or Frank's own hand; cross-links: [BENCH-1] (home instrument), [OPT-A]/[OPT-SIMD] (gap consumers), [ENG-PGO] (hand code embodies workload knowledge = what PGO should recover mechanically), D52's pcrec-bench (a hand-C testee triple is also natural THERE later; the in-repo arm is the fast-iteration instrument). (gate re-pointed 2026-09-22: [BENCH-1] folded into [OPTLOOP]; the home-instrument role is now the bench repo directly plus an [OPTLOOP] cycle's analysis, e.g. [OPTLOOP.1.analysis], docs/dev/optloop/cycle1_analysis.md)
- [EMIT-ALIGN] STATE:not-started — code alignment in the emitted artifact
  filed: 2026-09-28 Frank
  status (2026-09-28): not started; FILED 2026-09-28 by Frank, boonies: "add it as a boonies item. Test it when 'all' our optimizations are in" NOT TRIGGERED by O-68's bimodality — bench O-69 @ pcrec-bench e6dfbf6, answering I-116: the per-process split is the CPU FREQUENCY GOVERNOR at launch, not layout.…
  trigger: LAST, beside [OPT-SIMD] — when the algorithmic optimization queue is in (Frank: "when 'all' our optimizations are in"), so it is measured on the final code, not tuned against layouts that will move.
  refs: docs/dev/optloop/b108_reading.md; decisions D122 D137; known_issues K24
  ORIGIN: the [B108] placement twin (pcrec-bench O-67, docs/dev/optloop/b108_reading.md addenda) — under `-falign-functions=64 -falign-loops=64` S2a's named-population misses fell inside the null band (aws thr 1.036→1.014, lp-removed thr 1.117→1.029), i.e. the misses were PLACEMENT. That clears S2a; it does NOT show alignment makes anything faster in absolute terms (the twin compared arms, not aligned-vs-unaligned). QUESTION: does emitting alignment for the hot sites win broadly, beyond the null band, at what size cost?
  SPELLINGS on file: functions — `__attribute__((aligned(N)))` on the emitted entry/body functions (gcc+clang, survives into the user's link; the clean half); loops — no clean per-loop spelling: `-falign-loops` is a caller CFLAGS we do not own (K24's lesson: fixes go in the emitted text), `#pragma GCC optimize`/`__attribute__((optimize(...)))` is documented not-for-production (can reset other flags; clang ignores it), inline-asm `.p2align` at loop heads is fragile beside computed goto. SHAPE if it pays: an emission-wide form row with its own deny flag (D122 addendum 2), an abi event, measured over the WHOLE bench and both ISAs (x86 + aarch64), size priced on the dial (up to N-1 pad bytes per aligned site) — never a per-pattern or per-cell tweak (placement is a lottery that moves with every unrelated change).
  TRIGGER: LAST, beside [OPT-SIMD] — when the algorithmic optimization queue is in (Frank: "when 'all' our optimizations are in"), so it is measured on the final code, not tuned against layouts that will move. FIRST STEP then: absolute aligned-vs-unaligned per arm across capability (a bench reading; the O-67 builds are the precedent).
- [ENC-MODEL] STATE:not-started — THE FULL ENCODING MODEL
  filed: 2026-09-06 Frank
  status (2026-09-06): not started; CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-06, fifty-sixth session, boonies — "formalize encoding ascii as an encoding ... byte encodings would be translation tables"
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: src/gen/enc/; decisions D58 D77
  pattern encoding vs subject encoding separated, on the architecture that already exists. (1) THE PATTERN SEMANTIC SPACE IS UNICODE, canonically utf8 source — this is DD-12(1) as ruled (one parser, no encoding in the grammar, CharSet code-point intervals, encoding is a LOWERING instance), restated as the model's spine; [UTF-PAT] (raw utf8 pattern text contract) is its existing near-term half. (2) NON-UTF PATTERN SOURCES are a PRE-COMPILE CONVERSION WRAPPER (source-encoding -> utf8 before the parser), never a parser axis — Frank: "Best handled perhaps pre-compile ... it takes out this whole complication to a wrapper". Named risk to design against: misconverting rx OPERATORS — acute for multi-byte source sets whose trail bytes collide with ASCII metacharacters (Shift-JIS class) and for EBCDIC; single-byte sets are near-trivial.
  (3) SUBJECT ENCODINGS are TRANSLATION-TABLE BACKENDS on the D58 seam: a single-byte encoding = a 256-entry byte->code-point table (standard mappings exist for ISO-8859-x/Windows-125x/etc.); the lowering intersects the pattern's code-point intervals through the table's image to byte sets; src/gen/enc/'s third-encoding recipe is the landing shape. ASCII FORMALIZED as the first such table encoding (identity on 0x00-0x7F, 0x80+ ill-formed per the encoding's own rules — which also gives \d/\w/POSIX classes their per-encoding statement Frank asked for). (4) THE OPEN RULING, recorded not decided: what \x{hex}/octal DENOTE under a table encoding — a Unicode code point (mapped through the table, refusing points outside its image) or a subject-encoding unit; the split is visible exactly where byte != code point (Windows-1252 0x80 = U+20AC).
  Frank leans subject-encoding for the byte-valued spellings; the stage-2 per-encoding range-check precedent (byte caps at 0xFF) is the nearest ruled neighbour. D77: no build before a consumer; the row opens on Frank's word, likely after M5.0 completes and [UTF-PAT] lands.
- [FOLD-BASE] STATE:not-started — DIACRITIC/BASE-CHARACTER FOLDING, the caseless architecture's second customer
  filed: 2026-09-06 Frank
  status (2026-09-06): not started; CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-06, fifty-sixth session, boonies, from reading encoding tables: "a flag analogous to i that folds those characters together for matching"
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: decisions D18 D23
  Two strengths, named apart: CANONICAL EQUIVALENCE (precomposed e-acute == e + U+0301 — one abstract character, two encodings; ICU/Java CANON_EQ is the precedent, Java's notoriously slow at match time) and FOLD-TO-BASE (e-acute == e-grave == e — accent-insensitive, collation primary strength; what Frank asked for; subsumes the first). PCRE2 has NEITHER — a beyond-PCRE2 capability in [M4-SUBST]'s mold, pcrec's own flagged namespace per SR-10. THE MECHANISM IS D23's, WIDENED: caseless folds at class CONSTRUCTION and emits a fold-blind automaton; base-fold is the same move over UCD canonical-decomposition data (vendored at M5.0 stage 3 regardless) — a literal widens to its base-variant set.
  The wrinkle that keeps it honest: COMBINING SEQUENCES (NFD subjects spell e-acute as TWO code points), so under the flag each literal lowers to [base-variants] followed by mark absorption (\p{M}*-shaped) — still regular, still compiled, which is the AOT wedge: the whole equivalence closure lands in the tables at compile time where runtime engines pay per-match normalization or CANON_EQ blowup. \p relation stated so nobody re-derives it: \p{M} gives the VOCABULARY (talk about marks), never the EQUIVALENCE — this flag is new capability.
  Caveats for the design round: locale sensitivity at the edges (Swedish aa-ring/o-umlaut are distinct LETTERS; Turkish dotless-i) — first form scopes to locale-agnostic UCD decomposition with the caveat stated, never collation tables; and the flag interacts with `i` (case fold and base fold compose — the combined closure is one constructor pass, not two mechanisms, per the general-mechanisms rule).
  MECHANISM OPTION, NOT A RULING (Frank proposed it same day and downgraded it same day — "Downgrade my alternations as an option not a ruling"; the design round weighs it against the direct constructor-widening shape): the fold DESUGARS each literal to the ALTERNATION of its variants — the fully general form, expressing multi-code-point variants no class can hold — and [OPT-ALTCLS] (already in the tree, running right after parse, whose stage 1 is exactly single-char-run -> class merging) recovers the efficient class form where recoverable. Zero new engine machinery; the feature is a constructor desugar plus the existing optimizer. The unbounded-mark-stacking nuance rides as one desugared branch of shape base-then-mark-absorption beside the enumerated variants — still alternation, still altcls-visible for its class parts.
  DEPENDS on stage 3's UCD vendoring + stage 4's fold-closure machinery (this is DD-1's constructor one axis over); D18 earn-its-axis; opens on Frank's word after M5.0 completes.
- [MULTI-PAT] STATE:not-started — COMPILE A PATTERN SET INTO ONE SCANNER ARTIFACT, the Hyperscan concept on pcrec's own DFA
  filed: 2026-09-06 Frank
  status (2026-09-06): not started; CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-06, fifty-sixth session, from the Hyperscan discussion: "note it as an unscheduled row and put it in the boonies"
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: decisions D3 D77
  A plain alternation of N search patterns already yields the union machine (the same construction Hyperscan starts from) but NOT its results — three semantic gaps, recorded so the row starts honest: (1) PATTERN IDENTITY — an alternation reports "a match", not which branch; the subset construction already knows (a state's accept derives from which branches' accept states are in the set) and pcrec's priority-DFA machinery exists precisely to ERASE that (D3 accept-pruning picks the preferred branch), so the extension is multi-accept TAGGING, a reporting discipline on the existing machine; (2) ALL MATCHES vs leftmost-first non-overlapping — Hyperscan reports every pattern's every match end, overlapping included; PCRE find-all resolves by priority and resumes past the match (foo|foobar on "foobar" reports foo and never foobar) — the all-ends walk is CHEAPER than today's (no reverse pass, no priority resolution; the SOM-is-expensive mirror of [OPT-2]'s reverse-pass finding), so the shape is
  a new all-ends reporting ENTRY beside search, never a change to search's semantics; (3) STREAMING — save/restore automaton state across buffer boundaries, a property a pure DFA has and today's one-shot artifacts do not expose. Scope: DFA-compilable subset only (faithful to Hyperscan's own restriction); pattern-set INPUT rides the .rxt/definitions machinery that exists; scale caveat stated up front — union determinization is where blowup lives and where Intel's real effort went (literal prefilter decomposition, Teddy/FDR); pcrec's subset-elems/state caps refuse large unions HONESTLY, so the first instantiation is tens of patterns, not Snort-scale.
  D77: no build before a measured need — a named consumer wanting "scan for any of these N patterns with IDs" is the trigger; the design pass then decides tagging layout, the entry's contract (end offsets only vs paid SOM), and whether streaming is in or out of the first slice. SIBLING ROW: [V-E] (multi-pattern COMPILATION UNITS) is the complementary half — V-E packages N independent matchers into one unit with rx_info as identity; this row fuses N patterns into ONE machine with set semantics. A real consumer likely wants both (V-E's unit surface carrying this row's set scanner as one member).
- [JSONL] STATE:not-started — JSONL AS AN APPLICATION DOMAIN, five tiers stated by Frank
  filed: 2026-09-08 Frank
  status (2026-09-08): not started; CHARTERED unscheduled by Frank 2026-09-08 late evening, boonies, from watching pcrecdev2's slow jsonl reader: "jsonl is something that this rx might parse very well... not only can it do it, but it would be about as fast as it could be done"
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: decisions D18 D77
  (1) legal-JSONL validator, (2) schema validator for a given schema, (3) general parser via CALLOUTS, (4) known-schema jsonl parser, (5) jsonl-to-csv/etc converter via replace. THE ANALYSIS THAT ORDERS THEM (manager, same night): jsonl's line-per-record property makes the LINE the matching unit, so the usual JSON-isn't-regular objection mostly dissolves; tier 4 LEADS — a fixed schema with bounded nesting is a nearly-regular line language compilable onto the DFA/hybrid rungs with capture slots as the field extractor, which is where the as-fast-as-possible claim is strongest AND where the NAMED CONSUMER lives (the bench's store/*.jsonl record-schema reader, measured slow by its own operator — the D77 trigger is already half-met; ask the bench for the reader's baseline numbers + the schema when the row opens).
  Tier 1 general legality needs module `recursion`'s (?1) machinery (exists; VM rungs — honest boundary: a hand parser competes there; DEPTH-BOUNDED validation unrolls back toward the fast rungs and real schemas are depth-bounded). Tier 5 rides [M4-SUBST]'s direction. Tier 3 needs a NEW drop-in module `callouts` (PCRE2-compatible surface; the AOT form compiles a callout to a DIRECT CALL in the emitted C — no interpreter indirection, a beyond-PCRE2-quality story and a D18 earn-its-axis candidate). Sibling rows: [MULTI-PAT] (a schema set as one scanner), [V-E] (shipping a set of schema matchers behind the organizer). Opens on Frank's word after a design pass; first instantiation = the bench reader's schema, benchmarked against their current python reader.
- [SPAN-ISEL] STATE:not-started — BIT-PARALLEL-ACROSS-POSITIONS MATCHING, the bitstream method [Cam14+] (Parabix/icgrep)
  filed: 2026-10-10 Frank
  status (2026-10-10): not started; FILED 2026-10-10 by Frank, session 106; WIDENED the same day to the bitstream locator; SAVED FOR LATER, after the low-hanging fruit (Frank)
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: studies/simd1; memfn/docs/ideas.md; memfn/docs/responses.md; decisions D42 D157
  one u64 per condition per 64-byte block (one bit per subject position, from SIMD class compares + movemask); Advance = shift with a carry into the next block; bounded repeats unroll (za{1,3}b: M1=Adv(Adv(Z)&A), M2=Adv(M1&A), M3=Adv(M2&A), E=Adv((M1|M2|M3)&B), ~15 ops per 64 bytes); unbounded loops use MatchStar ((M&C)+C)^C|M. OUTPUT = match END positions, so it is a LOCATOR in the locate x finish split (a finisher recovers leftmost start + captures); it belongs as a row in the start/locate selection table, never a parallel mechanism. Cost scales with pattern size per block, not per byte: the candidate where no rare literal exists (dense-class patterns: \d{3}-\d{4}, ipv4, bench O-8) and the DFA's ~0.37 ns/B latency chain binds; it loses on large patterns and big counted repeats.
  DISTINCT FROM the shift-and named for case (f) (engine_m4.md §8 / D42; studies/simd1 §12-B), which is the other transpose (pattern states as bits, sequential over bytes) — a design covers both. SPECIAL CASE, the original filing: a FIXED-LENGTH class span (only constant shifts, evaluated at one position) as a CONSTRUCTED instruction sequence: pcrec hands the kit a FACT (per-position byte sets, D157), a kit-side SELECTOR builds the check from the span's composition plus a per-ISA-tier COST MODEL (SWAR / SSE2 / SSSE3 / AVX2 / AVX-512BW+VBMI). Per-position rung ladder: one masked interval `((x&K)-L) <=u R` (computed per class by searching K; covers exact, caseless, \d, [a-z], [a-zA-Z]; [WORD-FOLD]'s cube form is its R=0 case) -> union of k intervals (kor under AVX-512 k-masks) -> nibble table (shufti) / vpermi2b.
  SPIKE SHAPE (offline, quiet-box-safe): census the bench's patterns (no-rare-literal + small Glushkov size for the locator; span length + rung per position for the fixed case); prototype the selector and cost model (constants DERIVED from per-uarch uop tables, never hand-set); count ops against today's chains. Timing only after, on targeted hardware (AVX-512 is dev-box only). Raw notes: memfn/docs/ideas.md 2026-10-10 "fixed-length class spans". FRANK 2026-10-10 (after reading [Cam14+]): (1) it is a DIFFERENT ENGINE TYPE, (2) it does NOT go through memfn, (3) it works well as a LOCATOR. Consequences: the bitstream form is a THIRD ENGINE in pcrec's own codegen (APPROACH.md §2 "Two Engines, One IR" needs an amendment when it is built, Frank's milestone call), compiled from the IR like the DFA emitter; the kit-side selector wording above is superseded for the general form.
  Whether the fixed-length special case or the per-block class-to-bitmask leaves are kit sites is decided at design time, not presumed. Locator shape: the end stream gives every match end; the REVERSED pattern run backward gives a start stream whose lowest bit is the leftmost start, then the existing finisher runs forward from it for PCRE's preferred end and captures.
  FRANK 2026-10-11 (to the kit, recorded per its notice in memfn/docs/responses.md): gather information, NO kit build time; UNSCHEDULED items under this row: the two read-only span surveys (strict spans, anchored cores, bounded tokens; summary memfn/docs/ideas.md 2026-10-11); the masked-range span form with negation (one XOR per lane); a DESIGNED SPIKE — hand-written span filter/verify vs today's search on loglines/uuid, hex32-id, iso-ts and bounded/dotted4, tier U, both layers, no pcrec change, run opportunistically on a quiet box. Sibling row: [VM-CLSMASK].
- [BITSTREAM-GPU] STATE:not-started — run the SPAN-ISEL bitstream program as a GPU kernel
  filed: 2026-10-10 Frank
  status (2026-10-10): not started; BOONIES; FILED 2026-10-10 by Frank, session 106: "wouldn't seamless gpu support be cool"
  trigger: the bitstream engine exists AND a consumer asks for bulk throughput.
  the [SPAN-ISEL] bitstream program is a straight-line DAG of bitwise ops, shifts and adds, a portable IR that could emit C SIMD at any width (SSE2/AVX2/AVX-512) or a GPU kernel. [Cam14+] already uses long-stream addition and cites GPU work. FORCES: pcrec's output is self-contained gcc C with no runtime dependency, while a GPU target needs a driver and toolchain; per-call latency (kernel launch, transfer) loses on pcrec's ns-per-call model, so the only plausible win is bulk scanning of large GPU-resident or batched corpora. TRIGGER: the bitstream engine exists AND a consumer asks for bulk throughput. No measurement lane.
- [PIC-ARM] STATE:not-started — TEST/MEASURE UNDER THE CONSUMER'S BUILD MODE
  filed: 2026-10-11 manager
  status (2026-10-11): not started; FILED 2026-10-11, manager, session 107, from [LOSS-REVIEW] lrvm C1
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: docs/dev/optloop/lossreview/fixes.md
  the bench builds artifacts `-O2 -fPIC` (shared objects), where an EXPORTED function cannot be inlined (semantic interposition), so every backreference compare is `call rx_span_match@PLT` — 79% of the VM-route loss (27.2 ms) — and nothing in pcrec's own harness builds with `-fPIC`, so it was invisible for months. Add a `-fPIC` arm (codegen/size, and a per-artifact check that no hot emitted helper is called through the PLT) to the suite or test-axes; consider `-fvisibility=hidden`-equivalent emission rules (internal linkage for every non-caller entry) as the general form. Fix candidate #1 of docs/dev/optloop/lossreview/fixes.md is the first instance.
- [SPELLING-INV] STATE:not-started — SPELLING INVARIANCE
  filed: 2026-10-11 Frank
  status (2026-10-11): not started; FILED 2026-10-11 by Frank, session 107; UNSCHEDULED
  trigger: the census finds divergences on realistic spellings (a bench or wild pattern whose respelling changes route or form).
  refs: decisions D27
  regex is declarative, so equivalent spellings of one language should compile to equally good artifacts; pcrec is tested and tuned on OUR spellings, while users write `\d\d\d` for `\d{3}`, `[0-9]` for `\d` (byte domain), `(a|b)` for `[ab]`, `abc|abd` for `ab[cd]`, redundant groups/`{1}`/nested quantifiers (`(a*)*`, `.*.*`), lazy where greedy is equivalent, etc. Some normalization already exists (altcls merge/factor, possessify, count collapse); this row asks where it is incomplete. PRINCIPLE: one IR normalization layer (general mechanism, never per-spelling special cases) so respellings converge before engine selection.
  CHEAP FIRST STEP (census, compile-side): a RESPELLING GENERATOR over the corpus + bench patterns (meaning-preserving rewrites in both directions), compile each pair, compare the normalized emitted program and stamps (engine, start form, prefilter, size); every divergence is a missed normalization, ranked by bench weight. Free oracle: answer identity across respellings is a D27-style check needing no expected output (also catches miscompiles). Bench: its capability variants mechanism (`variants.tsv`, `gen_variants.py`, empty today) could carry respelled testees for timing. TRIGGER: the census finds divergences on realistic spellings (a bench or wild pattern whose respelling changes route or form).
- [POS-PARALLEL] STATE:not-started — umbrella: the position-parallel half of the four-engine taxonomy
  filed: 2026-10-11 Frank
  status (2026-10-11): not started; FILED 2026-10-11 by Frank, session 107; NEEDS THOUGHT BEFORE PROCEEDING — a design study after [LOSS-REVIEW] phase 1, so reach is counted on real losing cells
  next: starts only on Frank's word; no row-specific trigger recorded
  refs: memfn/docs/ideas.md; decisions D146 D157
  UMBRELLA for the position-parallel half of the four-engine taxonomy (memfn/docs/ideas.md 2026-10-11: {sequential, position-parallel} x {state-set, per-candidate control flow} = DFA, backtracking VM, the bitstream LOCATOR [SPAN-ISEL], and the mask-walk FINISHER "VM2"). Members: [SPAN-ISEL], [VM-CLSMASK]. SHARED STAGE 1: classify bytes into one truth vector per class the program tests. LADDER: (1) the stage-1 primitive; (2) HYBRID consumers inside today's engines (VM class tests / run advance / attempt-loop skip by bit reads and ctz; DFA self-loop-state spans left by one ctz over the mask, the general form of scan-edge/VMSTRIDE), each a selection-table row with its own deny; (3) VM2; (4) the bitstream locator.
  FRANK'S FRAMING (2026-10-11): (a) THE KIT may own primitives such as the class-membership vector, scalar (SWAR) and SIMD forms, but THE ORGANIZATION IS PCREC'S (which classes, when classified, mask lifetime, the consumers; D146/D157 facts-only to the kit); (b) the kit's whole-token-in-a-register idea (ipv4's max width 15 fits one 16-byte load, the window classified once, everything after is bit arithmetic) is the window-sized case of (c); (c) TRUTH VECTORS OVER THE WHOLE SUBJECT let VM2 run a pattern such as ipv4 `(\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}` WITHOUT LOADING AN INDIVIDUAL SUBJECT BYTE: the pattern is POSSESSIFIED (disjoint adjacent classes, which pcrec's possessify pass already proves), so each quantifier is one `ctz(~D>>p)` run length plus a bounds check and each literal one bit test; captures are offsets.
  DESIGN QUESTIONS for the study: whole-subject vectors (n/8 bytes per class, one full pass even when the first match is early) vs lazy per-block masks (find-first on large subjects) — a regime choice behind one positional accessor; VM2 where possessification fails (backtracking over masks); the start-candidate rule as a mask op under leftmost-first (`1234.5.6.7` matches from `234`); the byte domain first (utf8 classes need a different stage 1); the kit/pcrec contract for stage 1; the trigger cell per rung.
- [VM-CLSMASK] STATE:not-started — THE VM READS CACHED PER-BLOCK CLASS TRUTH VECTORS instead of re-testing bytes
  filed: 2026-10-11 Frank
  status (2026-10-16): NOT SCHEDULED, info-gathering only, later under [OPTLOOP] (Frank, 10-16); timing-1 done (lane/maskbench f862f6ba3): ctz run finisher wins 2-4x, per-call lazy classify erases most of it
  trigger: (D77) TIMINGS — ipv4 (and a utf8/`\w` cell) through the VM with and without cached masks, mask consumed bit-at-a-time vs by `ctz`, on targeted hardware; the walk_survey load instrument (studies/walk_survey/) supplies coverage per block.
  refs: memfn/docs/ideas.md; studies/walk_survey/; decisions D77
  for each class the VM program tests in a loop (ipv4's `\d`), classify a 64-byte block once, lazily, the first time the VM touches it (SIMD compare + movemask, one u64 per class per block); later instructions and attempts in that block read a bit. Raw notes: memfn/docs/ideas.md 2026-10-11 "the VM reads precomputed class truth vectors" (lane/memfn-ideas3 a4eaf749). PLACEMENT (manager, with Frank): pcrec VM engine work, NOT a kit site — the masks are VM state held across instructions and attempts, and the class-to-mask LEAF is the same primitive as [SPAN-ISEL]'s bitstream leaves (SIBLING-OF-A-FAMILY: build the leaf once for both).
  FORCES: a single test gains nothing (`(D>>(p-blk))&1` vs `(unsigned)(c-'0')<=9`, plus a block-valid check); the prize is (1) RUNS (`\d{1,3}`/`\d+` advance by `ctz(~D>>p)`), (2) RE-VISITS (backtracking and the attempt loop re-testing the same positions), (3) EXPENSIVE classes (utf8 classes, large sets, `\w` tables). Costs: classifying a whole block when a prefiltered VM touches only a few positions; mask state in rx_run_state (frame growth was itself a measured gcc cost, ccdiff_step0.md); block-boundary handling. For dense-class no-rare-literal patterns like ipv4 the [SPAN-ISEL] LOCATOR may dominate (the VM would then only finish at known ends and rarely backtrack), so the two are designed together.
  TWO SEPARABLE BENEFITS (Frank, same day, correcting the manager's first trigger, which priced only re-visits): (a) BATCHING — one SIMD classify answers all 64 positions in a few ops, so it pays even with no re-tests when the VM tests most positions of a block (ipv4's attempt loop tests `\d` at nearly every start); (b) REUSE across re-visits. The metric is COVERAGE (positions tested per classified block), not the re-test ratio. The counter-force: reading one bit (shift+and) costs about what the scalar test costs, so the batch is fully cashed in when the VM consumes the mask with word ops — `ctz` to the next start candidate in the attempt loop, `ctz(~D)` over a run — or replaces an expensive class test.
  TRIGGER (D77): TIMINGS — ipv4 (and a utf8/`\w` cell) through the VM with and without cached masks, mask consumed bit-at-a-time vs by `ctz`, on targeted hardware; the walk_survey load instrument (studies/walk_survey/) supplies coverage per block. The [SPAN-ISEL] spike may add one cell: ipv4 through the VM with and without cached digit/dot masks.
  CENSUS (2026-10-16, lane clscand, docs/dev/clsmask_candidates.md, counts only): leaders are the `\w`-token family (`(\w+) \1`); ipv4 is NOT a candidate under auto (0.011 tests/B; its win belongs to [SPAN-ISEL]); the start skip is the largest class-test consumer (0.48 tests/B forced-VM); utf8 classes never reach a run site; timing candidates T1-T5 (memo §6); microbench study `studies/clsmask_bench/` is on lane/maskbench, timing pending tonight.
  DISCUSSION 2026-10-16 (Frank, session 114; memo §10): classify would be a NEW kit contract, input a byte-language DAG plus frequency facts; pcrec decides WHETHER to cache, the kit HOW to fill (a fill never declines); modes SIMD block / scalar block / per-position memo; caching may matter more than SIMD.

- [SUBST-STREAM] STATE:deferred — compiled substitution's STREAMING mode with a stamped holdback
  filed: 2026-10-26 manager (split from [M4-SUBST] at its close)
  status (2026-10-26): not built; [M4-SUBST] closed with core, interface and extended forms landed 9ae59f5d4; in-place and C callbacks have their own rows ([SUBST-INPLACE], [INLINE-C])
  trigger: streaming re-opens (Frank parked streaming 2026-10-23, confirmed 2026-10-26); then this mode with it.
  refs: docs/design/replace_design.md; docs/design/subst_template_design.md; plan_completed.md [M4-SUBST] charter (2); [DD-14.FB] BufSurface precedent
  Charter (Frank, 2026-09-06, quoted from [M4-SUBST]): "STREAMING, with the AOT-specific win: a streaming replace needs a HOLDBACK buffer for a partial match at the chunk boundary, and pcrec knows the pattern's maximum width AT COMPILE TIME (cwmax/maxw, already load-bearing for lookbehind) — so a bounded-width pattern's artifact STAMPS ITS OWN REQUIRED HOLDBACK CAPACITY (caller supplies a fixed buffer of stamped size, zero allocation), and an unbounded-width pattern REFUSES streaming mode honestly by name."

- [SR-7] STATE:deferred — flavours (families as named masks)
  filed: 2026-08-13 (first appearance in plan.md, git; author unrecorded)
  status (2026-08-13): deferred; the flavour column exists from SR-1 and stays dormant
  trigger: a second flavour-varying row earns the axis (D18's earn-its-axis rule; exactly one, `\v`, is known today).
  refs: tests/registry/; decisions D18
  FLAVOURS (families as named masks: `pcre2-10.46`, `pcre2-dfa`, `python-re`, `ere`). Deferred by D18's earn-its-axis rule applied to the front end: exactly ONE flavour-varying row is known (`\v`), so the selection machinery has a set of size one and no customer. The column exists from SR-1; it turns on when a second flavour earns it. Note `pcre2-dfa` is the ENGINE-capability axis expressed as a family, so this step and DD-7/M4 are related. BLOCKER TO PLAN FOR (R4 critic finding): `pcrec_registry_find(kind, sel)` takes no flavour argument and returns the first row matching a byte, so SR-1's "short chain for the rare flavour-varying byte" is not expressible in the shipped shape — SR-7 must change that signature. Today a duplicate selector is caught loudly by tests/registry/ rather than silently shadowing, so this is a design debt, not a live bug
